## ס (id: BDB06420)

ס, *Samekh*, fifteenth letter; used as numeral 60 in postB. Heb.

> \[Str5429,5432] † סְאָה **n. f.** (v. *infr.*) sₑ’āh, a measure of flour, grain, etc. (√ unknown; perh. foreign word; NH *id.;* Aram. סְאָה, סָאתָא, ܣܳܐܴܐ, ܣܶܐܴܐ, ܣܰܐܬܳܐ (Gk. σάτον, LewyFremdwörter 116 f));—abs. סְאָ־ה [2 K 7:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:1); [7:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:16), וּסֳאָ־ה v:[18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:18) (Baer Ginsb cf. Ges§ 10 h); du. סָאתַיִם [1 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:32) \+ 3 t.; pl. סְאִים [Gn 18:6](https://ref.ly/Gen18:6), [1 S 25:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:18); —alw. c. appos. of thing measured, [Gn 18:6](https://ref.ly/Gen18:6) (J), [1 S 25:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:18), [1 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:32), [2 K 7:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:1), [7:1](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:1), [7:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:16), [7:16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:16), [7:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:18), [7:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs7:18) (where appar. masc., perh. after anal. of other measures of capacity, הִין, תֹמֶר, כֹּר, etc., AlbrechtZAW xvi (1896\), 95\);—סַאסְּאָה [Is 27:8](https://ref.ly/Isa27:8) v. סאסא.—On size of sₑ’āh \= 1/3 ephah, \= 12\.148 *litres* (\= 10\.696 qts.), v. NowArch. i. 203 BenzArch. 183\.

> \[Str5430] † \[סְאוֺן ?] **n.\[m.]** sandal, boot of soldier (prob. loan\-word from As. *šênu, shoe, sandal* (of leather), DlHWB 634 WklTel Amarna, Gloss., whence (denom.) *šênu, put on sandals* Dll. c.; cf. Aram. סֵינָא, ܣܐܘܽܢܳܐ, ܣܽܘܢܳܐ *sandal* (vb. denom. ܣܶܐܢ); Eth. ሽኣእን፡ *sandal*);—prob. cstr. כָּל־סְאוֺן סֹאֵן [Is 9:4](https://ref.ly/Isa9:4) *every boot of one tramping* (abs. Du al).

> \[Str5431] † \[סָאַן] **vb.** prob. **denom.** tread, tramp;—only **Qal** *Pt.* סֹאֵן בְּרַעַשׁ [Is 9:4](https://ref.ly/Isa9:4) (v. foregoing).

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Kings 18:32; Genesis 18:6; Isaiah 27:8; 1 Samuel 25:18; 2 Kings 7:1; Isaiah 9:4; 2 Kings 7:18; 2 Kings 7:16
* **Associated Articles:** סֵאסֵא (ID: `BDB06424@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## סֵאסֵא (id: BDB06424)

\[Str5432] † \[סֵאסֵא] **vb. Pilpēl**, whence *Inf.* בְּסַאסְאָהּ (MT בְּסַאסְּאָה) [Is 27:8](https://ref.ly/Isa27:8) \= *by driving her* (it) *away* (conj. fr. ‖בְּשַׁלְּחָהּ), acc. to Hi Ew Di Du CheHpt Am RVm; \> \= בִּסְאָה סְאָה *by the sₑ’āh, the sₑ’āh*, i.e. (Ges§ 123 c, 133 k) *by exact measure* Vrss (not LXX), Ges De cf. AV RV, which is prob. Rabbin. conceit. (On format. cf. טאטא, and v. Ges§ 55 f. Sta§ § 112 a Anm. 2; 238\.)

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 27:8


## סָבָא (id: BDB06425)

\[Str5433] † \[סָבָא] **vb.** imbibe, drink largely (NH *id.;* Aram. סְבָא *id.* (rare); cf. As. *sabû, sesame\-wine* DlHWB;—Ar. سَبْأٌ *wine*, is prob. loan\-wd. and سَبَأَ *import foreign wine* denom., Frä157 f);—**Qal** *Impf.* 1 pl. cohort. נִסְבְּאָה [Is 56:12](https://ref.ly/Isa56:12); *Pt. act.* סֹבֵא [Dt 21:20](https://ref.ly/Deut21:20), [Pr 23:21](https://ref.ly/Prov23:21); pl. סובאים Ez [23:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:42) Kt (Qr סָבָאִים, v. infr.); cstr. סֹבְאֵי [Pr 23:20](https://ref.ly/Prov23:20); *pass.* סְבוּאִים [Na 1:10](https://ref.ly/Nah1:10); —*imbibe*, c. acc. שֵׁכָר [Is 56:12](https://ref.ly/Isa56:12); pt. act. \= subst. wine\-*bibber, drunkard* [Dt 21:20](https://ref.ly/Deut21:20), [Pr 23:21](https://ref.ly/Prov23:21) (both \+ זוֺלֵל); fully, סֹבְאֵי יָ\+ין [23:20](https://ref.ly/Prov23:20) (\+ זֹלֲלֵי בָשָר); so also Ez [23:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:42) Kt (Qr perh. n. gent., so SyrVer AV, v. infr.), which rd. Sm RV SgfrKau Da; Co thinks dittogr. of מוּבָאִים, but perh. the reverse (ToyHpt om. מוּבָאִים): \> Berthol (q. v.) מִסְּבָא; other conj. v. in Sm; וּכְסָבְאָם סְבוּאִים [Na 1:10](https://ref.ly/Nah1:10) is prob. corrupt, pt. pass. of person dub., and sense obscure; del. both as dittogr. GunkelZAW xiii (1893\), 235 Now; om. in transl. We Kau. On [Ho 4:18](https://ref.ly/Hos4:18) v. סֹבֶא.

> \[Str5433] † \[סָבָא] only pl. סָבָאִים Ez [23:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:42) Qr \= *drunkards, wine\-bibbers* (?) so Thes al., but v. סְבָאִים.

> \[Str5435] † \[סֹ֫בֶא] **n. m.**:[Is 1:22](https://ref.ly/Isa1:22) drink, liquor;—only sf. סָבְאֵךְ [Is 1:22](https://ref.ly/Isa1:22); סָבְאָם [Ho 4:18](https://ref.ly/Hos4:18) (but v. infr.), [Na 1:10](https://ref.ly/Nah1:10) (but v. סָבָא );—*liquor* (appar. strong, choice) [Is 1:22](https://ref.ly/Isa1:22); ´סָר ס [Ho 4:18](https://ref.ly/Hos4:18) *their liquor* (i.e. their drunkenness) *is gone* Thes Hi Che RVm, but very dub., LXX quite diff. (ἡρέτισεν Ξαναναίους), We GuKau Now om. in transl.; conj. of HoutsmaTTijdschr ix (1875\), 60 סֹד סֹבְאִים, *a company of wine\-bibbers*, makes good sense.

> \[Str5434] † סְבָא **n. pr. m.** 1st son of Cush, poet. and late;—[Gn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7) (P) (LXX Σαβα, as שְׁבָא) \= [1 Ch 1:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:9) (LXX *id.*, but B Σαβατ); \= nation (or territory) [Psalm 72:10](https://ref.ly/Ps72:10) (LXX Σαβα; \+ שְׁבָא, LXX Αραβων), so (\+ כּוּשׁ, ‖מִצְרַיִם) [Is 43:3](https://ref.ly/Isa43:3) (LXX Σοηνη); clearly situated in south; most prob. \= λιμὴν Σαβά, and Σαβαὶ πόλις εὐμεγέθης, in Adulic gulf on W. coast of Red Sea; v. Straboxvi. 4\. 8\. 10 Ptoliv. 7, 7 f. DIGn BaePsalm DuIs; \> Meroë JosAnt. ii. 10, 2 al.

> \[Str5436] † סְבָאִים **n. pl. gent.** Sₑbā’im, LXX οἱ Σαβαειμ, A Σεβωειμ;—אַנְשֵׁי מִדָּה ´וּס [Is 45:14](https://ref.ly/Isa45:14) (\+ כּוּשׁ, ‖מִצְֹרַיִם, cf. [43:3](https://ref.ly/Isa43:3) supr.); this perh. intended also by Ez [23:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:42) Qr (v. סָבָא), perh. confounding it with שְׁבָא, cf. SyrVer.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 45:14; Proverbs 23:21; Ezekiel 23:42; 1 Chronicles 1:9; Nahum 1:10; Isaiah 56:12; Psalms 72:10; Isaiah 1:22; Isaiah 43:3; Proverbs 23:20; Hosea 4:18; Genesis 10:7; Deuteronomy 21:20
* **Associated Articles:** סָבָא (ID: `BDB06425@BDBHebrewLexicon`)

[Jos 19:14](https://ref.ly/Josh19:14). **2\.** pass. *be turned over* to (לְ), into the power of, [Je 6:12](https://ref.ly/Jer6:12). **Pi.** *Inf.cstr.* סַבֵּב אֶת־פְּנֵי הַדָּבָר [2 S 14:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:20) *to change, transform, the aspect of the matter*. **Poï.** *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יְסֹבְבֵנִי [Jon 2:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:4), [2:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:6), יְסֹבְבֶנְהוּ [Dt 32:10](https://ref.ly/Deut32:10), יְסוֺבְבֶנּוּ [Psalm 32:10](https://ref.ly/Ps32:10); 3 fs. תְּסוֺבֵב [Je 31:22](https://ref.ly/Jer31:22), etc.;—*encompass, surround* (poet. and chiefly late):—**1\.** *encompass* (with protection), c. acc. [Dt 32:10](https://ref.ly/Deut32:10) ( ´י subj.), c. 2 acc. חֶסֶד יְסוֺבְבֶנּוּ [Psalm 32:10](https://ref.ly/Ps32:10), cf. v:[7](https://ref.ly/Ps32:7) (but 2nd obj. here dub.); similarly נְקֵבָה תְּסוֺבֵב ָ\+גּבֶר [Je 31:22](https://ref.ly/Jer31:22) i.e. *either shall protect* (so most) or (Che, cf. Gf) the woman (fig. of Isr.), instead of holding aloof (הַבַּת הַשּׁוֺבֵבָה v:[22](https://ref.ly/Jer31:22)), will, in the new future which ´י creates, with affection *press round* her divine husband. **2\.** *come about, assemble round* (acc. Pers. ´י) [Psalm 7:8](https://ref.ly/Ps7:8). **3\.** *march* or *go about*, city (acc.) [Psalm 55:11](https://ref.ly/Ps55:11), [59:7](https://ref.ly/Ps59:7), [59:15](https://ref.ly/Ps59:15); altar (in solemn procession) [26:6](https://ref.ly/Ps26:6); *go about* in (בְּ) city Ct [3:2](https://ref.ly/Song3:2). **4\.** *enclose, envelop*, c. acc. Pers. [Jon 2:4](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:4), [2:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:6) (of waters).

(in) *the circuits of* the Nile \= *round about* the Nile, [Nu 11:24](https://ref.ly/Num11:24), [11:31](https://ref.ly/Num11:31), [11:32](https://ref.ly/Num11:32), [35:2](https://ref.ly/Num35:2) סְבִיבֹתֵיהֶם, Ju [7:18](https://ref.ly/Judg7:18), [1 S 26:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:5), [26:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:7), [2 K 6:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:17) סביבֹת אלישׁע, [Psalm 18:12](https://ref.ly/Ps18:12), [27:6](https://ref.ly/Ps27:6) על אֹיְבַי סביבותי, etc.; oft. idiom. preceded by אשׁר, as [Gn 33:5](https://ref.ly/Gen33:5) הערים אשׁר סביבותיהם [41:48](https://ref.ly/Gen41:48), [Lv 25:44](https://ref.ly/Lev25:44), [Nu 16:34](https://ref.ly/Num16:34), [Dt 6:14](https://ref.ly/Deut6:14), [13:8](https://ref.ly/Deut13:8), [17:14](https://ref.ly/Deut17:14), [21:2](https://ref.ly/Deut21:2) \+. ## סָבַךְ (id: BDB06438)

\[Str5440] † \[סָבַךְ] **vb.** interweave (‖ form to שבך q. v.);—**Qal** *Pt. pass.* pl. סִירִים סְבֻכִים [Na 1:10](https://ref.ly/Nah1:10) *interwoven* (entangled) *thorns* (cf. Da); GunkelZAW xiii (1893\), 235 prop. (after LXX Targum Vollers) כְּסוּחִים i.e. *cut off, away* ([Is 33:12](https://ref.ly/Isa33:12)) cf. Now; text very dub. **Pu.** *Impf.* 3 mpl. עַל־גַּל שָׁרָשָׁיו יְסֻבָּכוּ [Jb 8:17](https://ref.ly/Job8:17) *are interwoven* (in a tangled mass).

> \[Str5442] † סְבַךְ **n.\[m.]** thicket;—abs. בַּסְּבַח (נֶאֱחַז) [Gn 22:13](https://ref.ly/Gen22:13) (so Ginsb; Baer בַּסֲבַח, van d. H. כַּסְּבָךְ) a ram *caught in the thicket* by its horns; pl. cstr. סִֽבְכֵי הַיַּעַר [Is 9:17](https://ref.ly/Isa9:17) *thickets of the forest*, [10:34](https://ref.ly/Isa10:34).

> \[Str5441] † \[סְבֹךְ] **n.\[m.]** id.;—cstr. בִּֽסֲבָח־עֵץ [Psalm 74:5](https://ref.ly/Ps74:5) *in the thicket of trees;* sf. מִסֻּבְּכוֺ [Je 4:7](https://ref.ly/Jer4:7) (abode of lion; on בְּ v. Ges§ 20 h; on —ֻ Köii. 1, 512\).

> \[Str5444,7145] † סִבְּכַי **n. pr. m.** a captain of David; LXX Σοβοχαι, etc.; [2 S 21:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam21:18) \= [1 Ch 20:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr20:4), [1 Ch 11:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:29) \+ ‖ [2 S 23:27](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:27) where rd. ´ס for MT מְבֻנַּי Th We Dr Klo Bu Kit Löhr cf. HPS (B ἐκ τῶν υἱῶν, but LXXL Σαβενι); also [1 Ch 27:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:11).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 74:5; 1 Chronicles 20:4; Job 8:17; 2 Samuel 23:27; Nahum 1:10; Isaiah 9:17; 1 Chronicles 27:11; 1 Chronicles 11:29; 2 Samuel 21:18; Isaiah 33:12; Isaiah 10:34; Jeremiah 4:7; Genesis 22:13


## סָבַל (id: BDB06442)

\[Str5445] † \[סָבַל] **vb.** bear a heavy load (NH *id.;* Ar. סְבַל ܣܒܰܠ *id.;* cf. also sub זבל p. 259 b supr.);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. סְבָלָם [Is 53:4](https://ref.ly/Isa53:4); 1 pl. סָבָלְנוּ [La 5:7](https://ref.ly/Lam5:7); —*Impf.* 3 ms. יִסְבֹּל [Is 53:11](https://ref.ly/Isa53:11); 1 S. אֶסְבֹּל [46:4](https://ref.ly/Isa46:4); 3 mpl. sf. יִסְבְּלֻהוּ [46:7](https://ref.ly/Isa46:7); *Inf. cstr.* לִסְבֹּל [Gn 49:15](https://ref.ly/Gen49:15); —*bear* a load, וַיֵּט שִׁכְמוֺ לִסְבֹּל [Gn 49:15](https://ref.ly/Gen49:15) (poem in J; of Issachar under fig. of ass); of carrying an idol [Is 46:7](https://ref.ly/Isa46:7); of ´י carrying Isr. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Isa46:4), v:[4](https://ref.ly/Isa46:4); servant of ´י carrying load of pain [53:4](https://ref.ly/Isa53:4) and guilt v:[11](https://ref.ly/Isa53:11); Isr. bearing iniquities of fathers [La 5:7](https://ref.ly/Lam5:7). **Pu.** *Pt.* pl. מְסֻבָּלִים *laden*, [Psalm 144:14](https://ref.ly/Ps144:14) (i.e. pregnant Ges Hi Ew Hup\-Now Che al.; perh. better, token of abundant harvest, so many, v. esp. Bae). **Hithp.** *Impf.* וְיִסְתַּכֵּל הֶחָגָב [Ec 12:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:5) *drag oneself along*, as a burden (v. חָגָב).

> \[Str5447,5449] † סֵ֫בֶל **n.\[m.]** load, burden;—abs. [Ne 4:11](https://ref.ly/Neh4:11), [Psalm 81:7](https://ref.ly/Ps81:7) (enforced burden); cstr. *id.*, \= *burdensome labour* (of *corvêe*) [1 K 11:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:28).—Vid. also סַבָּל. p. 688

> \[Str5448] † \[סֹ֫בֶל] **n. m.**:[Is 10:27](https://ref.ly/Isa10:27) burden (always fig. of burden of tyranny);—only sf. סֻבְּלוֺ, [Is 10:27](https://ref.ly/Isa10:27) (v. reff. on סֻבְּכוֺ, \[סְבֹךְ]), ´עֹל ס [9:3](https://ref.ly/Isa9:3), [14:25](https://ref.ly/Isa14:25) (in all conceived as burden resting on shoulders).

> \[Str5449] † סַבָּל **n.\[m.]** burden\-bearer, (late);—only abs. ´ס coll. [Ne 4:4](https://ref.ly/Neh4:4), [2 Ch 2:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:1), [2:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:17); mpl. abs. סַבָּלִים [2 Ch 34:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:13); —נשֵֹׁא סַבָּל [1 K 5:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs5:29) is certainly wrong; LXX αἴροντες ἄρσιν, Vulgate *qui onera portabant*, hence prob. נשֵֹׁא סֵ֫בֶל; \> אִישׁ סַבָּל, as ‖ [2 Ch 2:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:1).

> \[Str5450] † \[סִבְלָה] **n. f.** burden;—pl. cstr. סִבְלֽת מִצְרַיִם [Ex 6:6](https://ref.ly/Exod6:6) (P), of the heavy labours imposed on Isr. by Egypt., v:[7](https://ref.ly/Exod6:7); sf. סִבְלֽתֵיכֶם [5:4](https://ref.ly/Exod5:4) (J), סִבְלֽתָם [1:11](https://ref.ly/Exod1:11), [5:5](https://ref.ly/Exod5:5) (both J), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Exod2:11) (E); (cf. [Psalm 81:7](https://ref.ly/Ps81:7), סֵבֶל).

> \[Str5451] † סִבֹּ֫לֶת **n. f.** prob. ear of wheat, etc., only Ju [12:6](https://ref.ly/Judg12:6) dial. form of II. שִׁבֹּלֶת q. v. (\> \= late wd. שִׁבֹּ֫לֶת *tide, flood*); cf. MarquardtZAW viii (1888\), 151 ff. but also GFM Bu.

> \[Str5453] † סִבְרַ֫יִם **n. pr. loc.** city between the border of Damascus and that of Hamath Ez [47:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek47:16); identif. by v. KasterenRevue Bibl. Internat. 1895, 23 ff. with *Khirbet Sanbariye* on the river Ḥâṣbâni, SW. of Hermon, cf. BuhlGeogr. 67, 238, but this appar. too far SW.; LXX Σεβραμ, A Σεφραμ. A city *Šabara’in* is named in Bab. Chron.:i. 28 (SchrKB ii. 276\); but location not given.

> \[Str5454] † סַבְתָּה, סַבְתָּא **n. pr. gent.** 3rd ‘ son ’ of Cush acc. to [Gn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7) (ה—ָ), \= [1 Ch 1:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:9) (א—ָ); identif. with Σαββαθα \[Periplus maris Erythr.:27], Σαυβαθα Ptolvi. 7, 38, or Σαβατα \[Straboxvi. 42], Sabota \[PlinNH vi. § 155, xii. 63], old commercial city of S. Arabia, by Tuch Ku, but this \= Sab. שבות (not ´ס) Levy\-OsZMG xix (1865\), 253; xx (1866\), 273, cf. HalJAS 7, iv. 525; GlaserSkizze ii. 252 f. prop. Σαφθα (Ptolvi. 7, 30\), near W. shore of Pers. Gulf; LXX Σαβαθα, Σαβατα, Σεβαθα. All uncertain conjectures.

> \[Str5455] † סַבְתְּכָא **n. pr. gent.** 5th ‘ son ’ of Cush acc. to [Gn 10:7](https://ref.ly/Gen10:7) \= [1 Ch 1:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:9) (ה—ָ acc. to Baer; א—ָ, as Gn, van d. H. Ginsb); location quite unknown; SyrVer Σαβακαθα, Σεβεκαθα.

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Kings 11:28; Nehemiah 4:11; Lamentations 5:7; 1 Kings 5:29; Exodus 5:5; 2 Chronicles 2:1; Isaiah 53:11; 1 Chronicles 1:9; Genesis 49:15; Ezekiel 47:16; Judges 12:6; Psalms 144:14; Exodus 6:7; Isaiah 53:4; Exodus 1:11; Genesis 10:7; Isaiah 9:3; Exodus 5:4; Isaiah 46:7; Isaiah 46:4; Psalms 81:7; Ecclesiastes 12:5; Isaiah 10:27; Nehemiah 4:4; Exodus 2:11; Isaiah 14:25; 2 Chronicles 2:17; Exodus 6:6; 2 Chronicles 34:13

## סָגַד (id: BDB06451)

\[Str5456] † \[סָגַד] **vb.** prostrate oneself in worship (only Is 44, 46) (perh. Aram. loan\-word in Heb., cf. NöZMG xli (1887\), 719; Aram. סְגֵב, ܣܓܶܕ, so OAram. סגד, ܣܓܕܐ (Sachau) Lzb328; Eth. ስገድ፡ all *id.;* Ar. سَجَدَ *be lowly, submissive, prostrate oneself in prayer*, etc., مَسْجِدٌ *mosque*, Nab. מסגדא *shrine* (?) Lzb152. 328, Syr. ܡܰܣܓܕܳܐ \= Ar.; cf. WeSkizzen iii. 165; Heid. 141\);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִסְגּוֺד לוֺ [Is 44:17](https://ref.ly/Isa44:17) Kt *he prostrateth himself to it* (an idol; Qr יִסְגָּד־לוֺ; \+ יִשְׁתַּחוּ, יִתְפַּלֵּל); וַיִּסְגָּד־לָמוֺ v:[15](https://ref.ly/Isa44:15) (‖וַיִּשְׁתָּחוּ); 1 S. לְבוּל עֵץ אֶסְגּוֺד v:[19](https://ref.ly/Isa44:19); 3 mpl. יִסְגְּדוּ אַף־יִשְׁתַּחֲווּ [46:6](https://ref.ly/Isa46:6) (abs.).—Cf. BAram. סְגִד.

> \[Str5509] סִגִים, סִגִּים v. סִיג sub I. סוג

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 44:17; Isaiah 46:6; Isaiah 44:19; Isaiah 44:15
* **Associated Articles:** I סוּג (ID: `BDB06474@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## סגל (id: BDB06453)

סגל (√ of foll.; cf. NH סְגוּלָּה as BH; סִגֵּל *acquire property;* Aram. סְגוּלָא, ܣܓܽܘܠܴܐ, *bunch of grapes*, As. *sugullâtê, herds;* also Ar. سَجْلٌ \[*a full bucket, bucketful*], *share, portion*).

> \[Str5459] † סְגֻלָּה **n. f.** possession, property (on format. v. BaNB § 95 b Köii. 1, 168\);—abs. ´ס [Ex 19:5](https://ref.ly/Exod19:5) \+ 5 t.; cstr. סְגֻלַּת [Ec 2:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:8); sf. סְגֻלָּתוֺ [Psalm 135:4](https://ref.ly/Ps135:4); —**1\.** *valued property, peculiar treasure*, which ´י has chosen (בחר) and taken to himself; always of people of Israel, first [Ex 19:5](https://ref.ly/Exod19:5) (E; LXX λαὸς περιούσιος \= [Tit 2:14](https://ref.ly/Titus2:14); 1 Pet 2:9 λαὸς εἰς περιποίησιν \= περιποίησις [Eph 1:14](https://ref.ly/Eph1:14) cf. BrMP 102; MA 52, 235\); then ´עַם ס [Dt 7:6](https://ref.ly/Deut7:6), [14:2](https://ref.ly/Deut14:2), [26:18](https://ref.ly/Deut26:18); later ´ס alone, [Mal 3:17](https://ref.ly/Mal3:17), [Psalm 135:4](https://ref.ly/Ps135:4). **2\.** *treasure* (very late), of kings [1 Ch 29:3](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:3) (gold and silver), [Ec 2:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:8).

> \[Str5461] † \[סָגָן, סֶ֫גֶן] **n. m.** prefect, ruler (loan\-word fr. As. *šaknu, prefect* of conquered city or province (√ *šakânu, set, appoint* DlHWB 659, cf. SchrCOT Is 41, 25; appar. \= NH סָגָן, סֶגֶן, Aram. סִגְנָא *a superior* (not *high*) *priest;* Mand. אשגאנדא WBrandtMand. Schriften 169 *a candidate for priesthood;* cf. Jen in Brandtib.; hence perh. Gk. ζωγάνης, cf. LewyFremdw. 129\);—only pl. סְגָנִים Ez [23:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:6) \+ 14 t.; sf. סְגָנֶיהָ [Je 51:57](https://ref.ly/Jer51:57) \+ v:[58](https://ref.ly/Jer51:58) (where rd. prob. סְגָנָיו LXX Gie);—**1\.** *prefects* of Assyr. and Bab. Ez [23:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:6), [23:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:12), [23:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:23), [Je 51:23](https://ref.ly/Jer51:23), [51:57](https://ref.ly/Jer51:57) (all \+ פַּחוֺת), [Is 41:25](https://ref.ly/Isa41:25), of king of Medes [Je 51:28](https://ref.ly/Jer51:28) (\+ פַּחוֺת). **2\.** *petty rulers, officials* of Judah (only Ne Ezr in sources): disting. from חֹרִים *nobles* [Ne 2:16](https://ref.ly/Neh2:16), [4:8](https://ref.ly/Neh4:8), [4:13](https://ref.ly/Neh4:13), [5:7](https://ref.ly/Neh5:7), [7:5](https://ref.ly/Neh7:5); alone, [2:16](https://ref.ly/Neh2:16), [12:40](https://ref.ly/Neh12:40), [13:11](https://ref.ly/Neh13:11); \+ יְהוּדִים [5:17](https://ref.ly/Neh5:17) (v. also [2:16](https://ref.ly/Neh2:16)); \+ שָרִים [Ezr 9:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:2). Cf. BAram. סְגַן.

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Peter 2:9; Psalms 135:4; Nehemiah 2:16; Deuteronomy 14:2; Nehemiah 4:13; Exodus 19:5; Jeremiah 51:23; Nehemiah 5:17; Nehemiah 4:8; Deuteronomy 7:6; Nehemiah 7:5; Nehemiah 13:11; Isaiah 41:25; Titus 2:14; Deuteronomy 26:18; Ecclesiastes 2:8; 1 Chronicles 29:3; Malachi 3:17; Ezekiel 23:6; Nehemiah 12:40; Jeremiah 51:28; Nehemiah 5:7; Jeremiah 51:58; Jeremiah 51:57; Ephesians 1:14; Ezekiel 23:23; Ezra 9:2; Ezekiel 23:12


## I סָגַר (id: BDB06456)

\[Str5462,4042,5462] I. סָגַר **vb.** shut, close (NH *id.,* Aram. סְגַר, ܣܓܰܪ *id.;* Zinj. מסגרת *prison* Lzb328; Ph. סגר Pi. or Hiph. *deliver over;* poss. Eth. ሸገርኣት፡ (prison) *guard* PräBAS i. 371\);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´ס [Gn 19:6](https://ref.ly/Gen19:6) \+, 3 pl. סָֽגְרוּ [2 Ch 29:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:7), סָֽגְרָוּ [Psalm 17:10](https://ref.ly/Ps17:10) (cf. Baer's n., Ges§ 29 o Köii, 1, 535 Anm.), סָגָרָוּ [Gn 19:10](https://ref.ly/Gen19:10), [Jos 2:7](https://ref.ly/Josh2:7); *Impf.* יִסְגֹּר [Jb 12:14](https://ref.ly/Job12:14), [Mal 1:10](https://ref.ly/Mal1:10), 1 pl. cohort. נִסְגְּרָה [Ne 6:10](https://ref.ly/Neh6:10), etc.; *Imv.* ms. סְגֹר [Psalm 35:3](https://ref.ly/Ps35:3), [Is 26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20) (וּסֲגֹר Baer Ginsb), mpl. סִגְרוּ [2 K 6:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:32); *Inf. cstr.* לִסְגּוֺר [Jos 2:5](https://ref.ly/Josh2:5); *Pt. act.* סֹגֵר [Is 22:22](https://ref.ly/Isa22:22); f. סֹגֶּרֶת [Jos 6:1](https://ref.ly/Josh6:1) (but v. infr.); *pass.* סָגוּר [1 K 6:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:20) \+ 12 t.;—**1\.** *shut* door (דֶּלֶת) [Gn 19:10](https://ref.ly/Gen19:10) (J) [2 K 6:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:32), [Mal 1:10](https://ref.ly/Mal1:10), [Ne 6:10](https://ref.ly/Neh6:10), [2 Ch 28:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:24), [29:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:7); gate (שַׁעַר) [Jos 2:7](https://ref.ly/Josh2:7) (JE) Ez [46:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:12), cf. [44:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:1), [44:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:2), [44:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:2), [46:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:1), לִסְגֹּר ´וַיְהִי הַשּׁ [Jos 2:5](https://ref.ly/Josh2:5) (JE), also (שַׁעַר om.) [Jos 6:1](https://ref.ly/Josh6:1) (si vera 1\.; סֹגֶרֶת dittogr. acc. to BuhlLex 13\); door after one (אַחֲרֵי), on leaving room [Gn 19:6](https://ref.ly/Gen19:6) (J); upon, behind, oneself, from within (בְּעַד q.v.) [2 K 4:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:4), [4:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:5), [4:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:33), [Is 26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20) (fig.), also (דֶּלֶת om.) Ju [9:51](https://ref.ly/Judg9:51), [2 K 4:21](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:21); c. דֶּלֶת \+ בְּעַד upon one left inside Ju [3:23](https://ref.ly/Judg3:23), and (דֶּלֶת om.) [Gn 7:16](https://ref.ly/Gen7:16) (J); fig. דַּלְתֵי בִטְנִי ´ס [Jb 3:10](https://ref.ly/Job3:10), cf. רַחְמָהּ ´ס [1 S 1:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:5), בְּעַד ´ס רַחְמָהּ v:[6](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:6); abs. *shut* (opp. פָּתַח) [Is 22:22](https://ref.ly/Isa22:22), [22:22](https://ref.ly/Isa22:22); metaph. ´חֶלְבָּמוֺ ס [Psalm 17:10](https://ref.ly/Ps17:10) *their fat* (i.e. gross, unreceptive heart) *they have closed*. **2\. a.** *close* in upon (בְּעַד) Ju [3:22](https://ref.ly/Judg3:22) (fat upon blade of sword), so poss. also וַיִּסְגֹּר בָּשָר תַּחְתֶּנָּה [Gn 2:21](https://ref.ly/Gen2:21) (J) *and flesh closed in*, in place of it (usu. and he closed flesh, i.e. closed the gap with flesh); עֲלֵיהֶם הַמִּדְבָּר ´ס [Ex 14:3](https://ref.ly/Exod14:3) *the wilderness hath closed in upon them;* obj. om., יִסְגֹּר עַל־אִישׁ [Jb 12:14](https://ref.ly/Job12:14) *he closeth in upon a man*, fig. of imprisonment. **b.** *close up* breach (פֶּרֶץ) \[in wall] of city [1 K 11:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:27); poss. *close* up \[path] (si vera 1\.) [Psalm 35:3](https://ref.ly/Ps35:3) Vrss Ol De Bae; JDMich DeW Ew Hup\-Now Che take סְגֹר here as weapon, usu. *battle\-axe* \[Gk. σάγαρις] of Massagetae Herodi. 215 cf. LagGes. Abh. 203, also Egypt. *sagartă* (loan\-word) Bondi55; both improb., text prob. corrupt; SchwallyZAW xi (1891\), 258 reads חֲגֹר \> HalRev. Sêm. iii (1894\), 47 עוּרָה (cf. [Psalm 59:5](https://ref.ly/Ps59:5)). **3\.** *Pt. pass., closed up* \= *closely joined* with tight seal, [Jb 41:7](https://ref.ly/Job41:7) (of scales of crocodile). **4\.** elsewhere only *Pt. pass.* in זָהָב סָגוּר (gold *shut up*, and so *prized, rare, fine* ?), only of temple adornment and utensils [1 K 6:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:20), [6:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:21), [7:49](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:49), [7:50](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:50), [10:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:21) \= [1 Ch 9:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:20), [1 Ch 4:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:20); [4:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:22); perh. read סָגוּר (abbrev. for ´ס ´ז) [Jb 28:15](https://ref.ly/Job28:15) (for MT סְגוֺר, so Hoffm Bu Du): cf. As. *ḫurâṣu sakru*, DlHWB 499\.

> \[Str5458] † סְגוֺר **n.\[m.]** enclosure, encasement;—cstr. סְגוֺר לִבָּם [Ho 13:8](https://ref.ly/Hos13:8) *the encasement of their heart* (*pericardium;* i.e. their vitals).—סְגוֺר [Jb 28:15](https://ref.ly/Job28:15) must \= *fine gold*, si vera l., but v. סגר, **Qal.** ad fin.; סְגֹר [Psalm 35:3](https://ref.ly/Ps35:3) v. *id.,* **Qal 2 b.**

> \[Str5474] † סוּגַר **n.\[m.]** cage, prison (poss. loan\-word from As. *sûigaru, cage* (BaNB 22\); NH סוּגָר *dog\-collar* or *chain* \= Syr. ܣܽܘܓܳܪܳܐ (*clog of*) *dog\-collar;* whence Ar. سَاجُورٌ *dog\-collar* (as loan\-word), Frä114 PräBAS i. 372\);—וַיִּתְּנֻהוּ בַסּוּגַר Ez [19:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:9) *and they put him into a cage*.

> \[Str4525] † מַסְגֵּר **n.\[m.] 1\.** locksmith, smith. **2\.** dungeon:—alw. abs.: **1\.** coll. *smiths*, וְכָל־הֶחָרָשׁ ´וְהַמּ [2 K 24:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:14) cf. v:[16](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:16), [Je 24:1](https://ref.ly/Jer24:1), [29:2](https://ref.ly/Jer29:2). **2\.** *dungeon* [Is 24:22](https://ref.ly/Isa24:22) (cf. סגר **Pu.**); fig. of exile [42:7](https://ref.ly/Isa42:7) (´הוֺצִיא מִמּ); נַפְשִׁי ´הוֺצִיאָה מִמּ [Psalm 142:8](https://ref.ly/Ps142:8).

> \[Str4526] † מִסְגֶּ֫רֶת **n.f. 1\.** border, rim. **2\.** fastness;—abs. ´מ [Ex 25:27](https://ref.ly/Exod25:27), [37:14](https://ref.ly/Exod37:14); cstr. ´מ [25:25](https://ref.ly/Exod25:25), [37:12](https://ref.ly/Exod37:12); sf. מִסְגַּרְתּוֺ [25:25](https://ref.ly/Exod25:25); [37:12](https://ref.ly/Exod37:12); pl. abs. מִסְגְּרוֺת [1 K 7:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:28) \+; sf. מִסְגְּרֹתֶיהָ v:[35](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:35), v:[36](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:36), etc.;—**1\.** *border, rim* (enclosure), of sacred table in tabern. Benz), also [2 K 16:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:17). **2\.** *fastness*, ´וְיַחְרְגוּ מִמּ [Psalm 18:46](https://ref.ly/Ps18:46) \= [2 S 22:46](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:46) (v. חרג) *and they shall come quaking out of their fastnesses*, so [Mi 7:17](https://ref.ly/Mic7:17) (יִרְגְּזוּ, like reptiles), all of nations, in awe of ´י.

## סגר (id: BDB06461)

**II.** סגר (√ of foll.; cf. appar. Ar. سَجَرَ *fill with water* Lane1308, سَاجِرٌ torrent *that fills everything* Idib.; Syr. ܣܰܓܪܳܐ *imber vehemens*, and Sam. אַסְגָּר, cf. GeiNachgel. Schr. iv. 186\).

> \[Str5464] † סַגְּרִיר **n.\[m.]** steady, persistent rain (on format. cf. BaNB 215\);—abs. ´יוֺם ס [Pr 27:15](https://ref.ly/Prov27:15) *a day of steady rain, rainy day.*

> \[Str5465] † סַד **n.\[m.]** stocks, for confining feet of culprits (prob. loan\-word fr. Aram. סַדָּא, סַדָּנָא ܣܰܕܳܐ; NH סַד, pl. סַדִּין); \= Lat. *nervus*, Gk. ποδοκάκη;—abs. רַגְלַיִם´שִים בַּמּ [Jb 13:27](https://ref.ly/Job13:27) (LXX κώλύμα), [33:11](https://ref.ly/Job33:11) (SyrVer ξύλό); (cf. syn. מַהְפֶּכֶת, and Gk. equiv. there cited).

> \[Str5467] סְדֹּם:39 **n.pr.loc.** Sodom, important Canaanitish city named (usu.) with Gomorrha (עֲמֹרָה, q.v.);—LXX Ζόδομα (inflected Ζοδόμώ, Ζοδομοις ):—´ס (on format. cf. LagBN 54\), [Gn 13:10](https://ref.ly/Gen13:10), [13:12](https://ref.ly/Gen13:12), [13:13](https://ref.ly/Gen13:13) \+ 7 t. Gn 18, 19, \+ מְדֹ֫מָה (ה loc.) [Gn 10:19](https://ref.ly/Gen10:19), [18:22](https://ref.ly/Gen18:22), [19:1](https://ref.ly/Gen19:1) (all J), \+ 8 t. Gn 14; fr. 8th cent. onwards, used as illustrating ´י's judgments, [Am 4:11](https://ref.ly/Amos4:11), [Is 1:9](https://ref.ly/Isa1:9), [13:19](https://ref.ly/Isa13:19), [Dt 29:22](https://ref.ly/Deut29:22), [Je 49:18](https://ref.ly/Jer49:18), [50:40](https://ref.ly/Jer50:40), [Zp 2:9](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:9), [La 4:6](https://ref.ly/Lam4:6); as proverbial for open sin [Is 3:9](https://ref.ly/Isa3:9), [Je 23:14](https://ref.ly/Jer23:14) so metaph. ´קְצִינֵי ס [Is 1:10](https://ref.ly/Isa1:10) (i.e. rulers as corrupt as in Sodom), ´גֶּפֶּן ס [Dt 32:32](https://ref.ly/Deut32:32) (i.e. wickedness like Sodom's); Judah cp. with ´ס to her disadvantage Ez [16:46](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:46), [16:48](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:48), [16:49](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:49), [16:53](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:53), [16:55](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:55), [16:56](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:56). Site prob. at S. end of Dead Sea, where are now *Jebel Usdum* (SW.), and *Zoar* (SE.) cf. Di:Gn 19, 20 ff. RobBR ii. 187 ff. GASmGeogr. 505 ff. BlankenhornZPV xix (1896\), 53 ff BDPal. 3, 146 BuhlGeogr. 117, 271, 274\. Vid. also שִדִּים.

> \[Str5466] † סָדִין **n.\[m.]** linen wrapper (perh. foreign word; cf. As. *sudinnu* DlHWB 490, a garment; NH סָדִין, Targum סְדִינָא, Syr. ܣܕܝܢ, ܣܕܝܢܐ (rare) [Mk 15:46](https://ref.ly/Mark15:46); \> cf. Ar. سِدْنٌ, سَدِينٌ *veil, saddle\-cover* (v. Lane1335), or ܣܶܕܽܘܢܳܐ, Gk. \[and LXX] σινδών, cf. Frä48 LewyFremdw. 85\);—abs. ´ס [Pr 31:24](https://ref.ly/Prov31:24); pl. סְדִינִים Ju [14:12](https://ref.ly/Judg14:12), [14:13](https://ref.ly/Judg14:13), [Is 3:28](https://ref.ly/Isa3:28); —*wrapper* or rectangular piece of fine *linen*, worn as outer, or (at night) as sole garment (cf. GFMJu 14, 12\), \+ חֲלִפֹּת בְּגָדִים Ju [14:12](https://ref.ly/Judg14:12), [14:13](https://ref.ly/Judg14:13), in list of women's finery [Is 3:23](https://ref.ly/Isa3:23), made and sold by the capable woman [Pr 31:24](https://ref.ly/Prov31:24).

## סדר (id: BDB06466)

סדר (√ of foll.; cf. As. *sadâru, arrange in order, sidru, sidirtu, row, battle\-line;* NH סָדַר *arrange, order,* Aram. סְדַר, ܣܕܰܪ, all c. deriv.).

> \[Str5468] † \[סֵ֫דֶר] **n.\[m.]** arrangement, order (on vocaliz. cf. As. *sidru, sidirtu,* and BaNB § 77 c); \- only pl. לאֹ סְדָרִים [Jb 10:22](https://ref.ly/Job10:22) \= *disorder, confusion,* of the dark underworld.

> \[Str7713] † \[שְדֵרָה] **n.f. 1\.** row, rank of soldiers in line. **2\.** architectural term. (prob. for סְדֵרָה, v. supr. and cf. Ba:§ 92 a);—only pl. abs. שְדֵרוֺת [2 K 11:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:8), [2 Ch 23:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:14), רֹת\- [1 K 6:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:9), [2 K 11:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:15); —**1\.** *rows, ranks,* [2 K 11:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:8), and v:[15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:15) \= [2 Ch 23:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:14) (where thought to be gloss by Benz, on account of מִבַּיִת, *within*). **2\.** term. techn. of building, גֵּבִים וּשְדֵרֹת בָּאֲרָוִים [1 K 6:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:9), meaning unknown.

Šab\-’\-ê \= סֶוֶא with Sûabaku, founder of 25th (Ethiop.) dynasty, cf. WiedÄg. Gesch. 583 f.; very uncertain is Wkl's conj. of סוא \= *Sib\-’\-ê* as general of kg. *Pir’u* of *Muṣri* in N Arab. [Am 3:15](https://ref.ly/Amos3:15); *Impf.* 3 ms. יֶסוּף [Est 9:28](https://ref.ly/Esth9:28); 3 mpl. יָסֻ֫פוּ [Is 66:17](https://ref.ly/Isa66:17); —*come to an end* [Am 3:15](https://ref.ly/Amos3:15), [Is 66:17](https://ref.ly/Isa66:17), [Psalm 73:19](https://ref.ly/Ps73:19) (\+ תַּמּוּ), וְזִכְרָם לאֹ־יָסוּף מִזַּרְעָם [Est 9:28](https://ref.ly/Esth9:28). **Hiph.** *make an end of,* only (if text correct) *Impf.* 1 s. (\+Inf. abs. אָסֹף, chosen for assonance, v. אסף), cohort. \= juss. In form, אָסֹף אָסֵף [Zp 1:2](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:2), אָסֵף v:[3](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:3), v:[3](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:3), אָסֹף אֲסִיפֵם [Je 8:13](https://ref.ly/Jer8:13) (on these forms v. Gf Bö§ 988, 1 Köi.445, 466\); but rd. perhaps, for אָסֵף, אֶאֱסֹף, We BuhlLex 13, or אֹסֵף, Ges§§ 72 aa, 113 w n.3 Now:[Zp 1:2](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:2), and in Je אָסֹף אֹסְפֵם Gesl.c. (against Gie v. Hi).

> \[Str5488] † I. סוּף **n.m.**:[Jon 2:6](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:6) reeds, rushes (coll.) (prob. loan\-word from Egypt. *ṭwfi, reeds,* SteindorffBAS i. 603 ErmanZMG xivi (1892\), 122; Semitic acc. to WMMAs.u.Eur. 101\);—**1\.** *rushes,* in Nile [Ex 2:3](https://ref.ly/Exod2:3), [2:5](https://ref.ly/Exod2:5) (E); קָנֶה וָסוּף [Is 19:6](https://ref.ly/Isa19:6) (of Egypt). **2\.** usu. in combin. יַם־סוּף prob. \= *sea of rushes or reeds* ( \> *sea of* (city) *Suph*), which Gk. incl. in wider name θάλασσα Ἐρυθρἀ, **Red Sea** (cf. Di:[Ex 13:18](https://ref.ly/Exod13:18) and esp. WMMAs. u. Eur. 42f., who expl. as name orig. given to upper end of Gulf of Suez, extending into Bitter Lakes, shallow and marshy, whence *reeds* (prob. also reddish colour));—name applied only to arms of Red Sea; most oft. **a.** to *Gulf of Suez* [Ex 10:19](https://ref.ly/Exod10:19), [Jos 2:10](https://ref.ly/Josh2:10) (both J), [Ex 13:18](https://ref.ly/Exod13:18), [15:4](https://ref.ly/Exod15:4), [15:22](https://ref.ly/Exod15:22), [23:31](https://ref.ly/Exod23:31) (all E), [Dt 11:4](https://ref.ly/Deut11:4), [Jos 4:23](https://ref.ly/Josh4:23) (D), [Nu 33:10](https://ref.ly/Num33:10), [33:11](https://ref.ly/Num33:11) (P), elsewh. late [Ne 9:9](https://ref.ly/Neh9:9), [Psalm 106:7](https://ref.ly/Ps106:7), [106:9](https://ref.ly/Ps106:9), [106:22](https://ref.ly/Ps106:22), [136:13](https://ref.ly/Ps136:13), [136:15](https://ref.ly/Ps136:15). **b.** sts. to *Gulf of Akaba* [1 K 9:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:26), and דֶּרֶךְ יַם־סוּף [Nu 21:4](https://ref.ly/Num21:4) (E), prob. also [14:25](https://ref.ly/Num14:25) (E), [Dt 1:40](https://ref.ly/Deut1:40), [2:1](https://ref.ly/Deut2:1); perh. Ju [11:16](https://ref.ly/Judg11:16), [Je 49:21](https://ref.ly/Jer49:21); poss. rd. מִיַּם־סוּף for ´מוֺל ס [Dt 1:1](https://ref.ly/Deut1:1) (v. infor.).

> \[Str5489] †II. סוּף **n.p.r.loc.** (si vera l.) named in defining loc. of Deut. law\-giving [Dt 1:1](https://ref.ly/Deut1:1) (מול סוּף, where מוֺל by dissimil. for מוּל); but read perh. מִיַּם סוּף (LXX πλησίον ρῆς ἐρυθρᾶς, LXXL \+ θαλάσσης, Vulgate *contra mare rubrum*), v. I. סוּף.

> \[Str5492] † II. סוּפָה **n.p.r.loc.** E. of Jordan ;—only in phr. ´וָהֵב בְּס in ancient poet. fragment [Nu 21:14](https://ref.ly/Num21:14); TristrMoab 50 cp. Sāfieh (صافيه), SE. oasis of Dead Sea, but ס \= ص is most improb.

> \[Str5493–94] I. סוּר adj. verb. properly *Pt.* of סור q.v.

> \[Str5495] †II. סוּר **n.pr.** of a temple\-gate;—´שַׁעַר ס [2 K 11:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:6) (\> ‖ [2 Ch 23:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:5) שַׁעַר הַיְסוֺד, v. יְסוֺד), but del. ver. as gloss WeBleek, Einl. 4, 258 KmpKau Benz.

> \[Str5627] † סָרָה **n.f.** a turning aside, defection, apostasy, withdrawal;—alw. abs. ´ס;—**1\.** *defection*, used appar. of any moral or legal offence [Dt 19:16](https://ref.ly/Deut19:16), so prob. [Is 59:13](https://ref.ly/Isa59:13) (\+ עשֶׁק, דִּבְרֵי שָׁקֶר). **2\.** *apostasy*, [Dt 13:6](https://ref.ly/Deut13:6), [Is 1:5](https://ref.ly/Isa1:5), [31:6](https://ref.ly/Isa31:6), [Je 28:16](https://ref.ly/Jer28:16), [29:32](https://ref.ly/Jer29:32). **3\.** סָרָה בִּלְתִּי *non\-withdrawal* [Is 14:6](https://ref.ly/Isa14:6) (cf. vb., v:[25](https://ref.ly/Isa14:25), [Am 6:7](https://ref.ly/Amos6:7)).

## סוּת (id: BDB06509)

\[Str5496] † \[סוּת] **vb. Hiph.** incite, allure, instigate (NH Hiph., *id.*);—**Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. sf. הֱסִֽיתְ [1 S 26:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:19), הֲסֵיתְ [Jb 36:16](https://ref.ly/Job36:16); 3 fs. הֵסַ֫תָּה [1 K 21:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:25) (Ges§ 72 w Köi. 460\); 3 pl. sf. הִסִּית֫וְּ [Je 38:22](https://ref.ly/Jer38:22) (Ges§ 72 ee); *Impf.* 3 ms. יַסִּית [2 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:32) \+ 2 t., but also וַיָּ֫סֶת [2 S 24:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:1), [1 Ch 21:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:1), sf. יְסִֽיתְ [Dt 13:7](https://ref.ly/Deut13:7), [Jb 36:18](https://ref.ly/Job36:18), etc.; *Pt.* מַסִּית [Je 43:3](https://ref.ly/Jer43:3), [2 Ch 32:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:11); —**1\. a.** *incite* to make a request (c. acc. Pers. \+ inf.) Ju [1:14](https://ref.ly/Judg1:14) (on text v. GFM) \= [Jos 15:18](https://ref.ly/Josh15:18). **b.** *allure* מִמֶּנּוּ וַיְסִיתֵם [2 Ch 18:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:31) *and* God *allured them away fr. him* (si vera l.; del. cl. as gloss Be Kit); so also [Jb 36:16](https://ref.ly/Job36:16) acc. to De Hi Bu al.; *he allureth thee* out *of the mouth of distress*, but Di Du *freedom hath seduced thee;* for meaning *seduce, entice,* cf. also v:[18](https://ref.ly/Job36:18). **2\.** *instigate*, in bad sense, c. acc. Pers., \+ בְּ *against*, [1 S 26:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:19), [2 S 24:1](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:1), [Je 43:3](https://ref.ly/Jer43:3), [Jb 2:3](https://ref.ly/Job2:3); c. acc. Pers. alone [1 K 21:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:25), [2 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:32) \= [Is 36:18](https://ref.ly/Isa36:18), cf. [2 Ch 32:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:15), [Dt 13:7](https://ref.ly/Deut13:7) [Je 38:22](https://ref.ly/Jer38:22); \+ inf. [1 Ch 21:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:1), [2 Ch 18:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:2), [32:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:11).

> \[Str5497] סוּת **n.** v. סוה

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Chronicles 32:15; Deuteronomy 13:7; Jeremiah 43:3; 2 Samuel 24:1; Isaiah 36:18; 2 Chronicles 32:11; 1 Samuel 26:19; Jeremiah 38:22; 1 Chronicles 21:1; Judges 1:14; Joshua 15:18; Job 36:16; 2 Kings 18:32; 2 Chronicles 18:31; Job 2:3; Job 36:18; 1 Kings 21:25; 2 Chronicles 18:2
* **Associated Articles:** סוה (ID: `BDB06481@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## סָחַב (id: BDB06511)

\[Str5498] †\[סָחַב] **vb.** drag (*Impf.* consec. 1 s. ואסחב MI18, sf. ואסחבה Ib12.13; Ph. סחב Lzb328; Ar. سَحَبَ, Eth. ሰሐበ፡);—**Qal** *Pf.* 1 pl. וְסָחַ֫בְנוּ consec. [2 S 17:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:13); *Impf.* 3 ms. sf. יִסְחָבוּם [Je 49:20](https://ref.ly/Jer49:20), [50:45](https://ref.ly/Jer50:45); *Inf. abs.* סָחוֺב [Je 22:19](https://ref.ly/Jer22:19); *cstr.* לִסְחֹב [Je 15:3](https://ref.ly/Jer15:3); —*drag*, a city עַד־הַנַּחַל [2 S 17:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:13), corpses [Je 15:3](https://ref.ly/Jer15:3), [22:19](https://ref.ly/Jer22:19), captives, under fig. of sheep dragged off by wild beast [49:20](https://ref.ly/Jer49:20) \= [50:45](https://ref.ly/Jer50:45) (LXX pass., whence Schwally Gie suppose a **Niph.** יִסָּחֲבוּ).

> \[Str5499] † סְחָבָה **n.f. rag, clout** (stuff *pulled* or *dragged about;* \> LagBN 143 √ شَحُبَ, شَحَبَ *become altered for the worse*);—only pl. סְחָבוֺת [Je 38:11](https://ref.ly/Jer38:11), [38:12](https://ref.ly/Jer38:12) (both \+ מְלָחִים).

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 15:3; 2 Samuel 17:13; Jeremiah 22:19; Jeremiah 49:20; Jeremiah 38:12; Jeremiah 38:11; Jeremiah 50:45


## סָחָה (id: BDB06513)

\[Str5500] † \[סָחָה] **vb. scrape** (Ar. سَحَا (سحو, and سحى Lane1322) *scrape off, clear away;* cf. Talm. סְחוּתָא, סְחִיתָא *refuse*, Targum סְחִיתָא *dirt, dung*);—only **Pi.** scrape clean, scour, *Pf.* 1 S. consec. וְסִחֵתִ֫י עֲפָרָהּ מֶּנָּהָ Ez [26:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek26:4) *and I will scrape clean her dust from her*.

> \[Str5501] † סְחִי **n.\[m.]** offcouring;—וּמָאוֺס תְּשִימֵנוּ ´ס [La 3:45](https://ref.ly/Lam3:45).

> \[Str7823] † סָחִישׁ **n.\[m.]** grain that shoots up of itself in 2nd year (√ unknown);—‖סָפִיחַ, [2 K 19:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:29) (\= שָׁחִיס ‖ [Is 37:30](https://ref.ly/Isa37:30)); on use of this for food, cf. Straboxi. 4, 3 (of Albanians).

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 26:4; Lamentations 3:45; 2 Kings 19:29; Isaiah 37:30


## סָחַף (id: BDB06516)

\[Str5502] † \[סָחַף] **vb.** prostrate (NH סחף, Aram. סְחַף ܣܚܰܦ (for ἐδαφίζω, [Lk 19:42](https://ref.ly/Luke19:42)); As saḫâpu, *throw down, overwhelm*);—**Qal** *Pf.* מָטָר סֹחֵף [Pr 28:3](https://ref.ly/Prov28:3) *a prostrating rain* (beating down grain), וְאֵין לָחֶם *and* (so) *there is no bread*. **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. נִסְחַף אַבִּירֶיְ [Je 46:15](https://ref.ly/Jer46:15) *why are thy mighty ones* (or, *why is thy bull*, i.e. Apis, Hi Ew Gf) *prostrated?* but rd. perh. נָס חָף אַבִּירְ לאֹ עָמַד (LXX Mich Gie Co) *why hath Apis fled, thy bull doth not stand?*

* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 28:3; Jeremiah 46:15; Luke 19:42


## סָחַר (id: BDB06517)

\[Str5502] † \[סָחַר] **vb.** go around, about, travel about in (NH *id., go about as merchant, pedlar*, perh. denom. from סוֺחֵר (Jastr); Syr. ܣܚܪ *go about as beggar, be beggar* PS2593 (rate), esp. Chr Pal Aram. v. SchwallyIdiot. 61 f.; As. saḫâru, *turn, surround*, Tel Am. *return* WklTA Gloss.; Targum *surround* (oft.), and deriv.; Ar. سَخَرَ is *mock at, deride*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. סָֽחֲרוּ [Je 14:18](https://ref.ly/Jer14:18); *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִסְחֲרוּ [Gn 34:21](https://ref.ly/Gen34:21); 2 mpl. תִּסְחָרוּ [Gn 42:34](https://ref.ly/Gen42:34); *Imv.* mpl. sf. סְחָרוּהָ [Gn 34:10](https://ref.ly/Gen34:10); *Pf.* סֹחֵר [Gn 23:16](https://ref.ly/Gen23:16), סוֺחֵר [Pr 31:14](https://ref.ly/Prov31:14); cstr. סֹחֵר [Is 23:2](https://ref.ly/Isa23:2); fs. sf. סֹחַרְתֵח Ez [27:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:12), [27:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:16), [27:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:18) \+ סֹחֲרֹתַיִח v:[15](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:15) (v. infr.); mpl. סֹחֲרִים [Gn 37:28](https://ref.ly/Gen37:28) \+ 2 t.; cstr. סֹחֲרֵי [1 K 10:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:28) \+ 3 t.; sf. סֹחֲרַיִח [Is 47:15](https://ref.ly/Isa47:15), רָיִח\- Ez [27:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:21), רֶיהַ\- [Is 23:8](https://ref.ly/Isa23:8); —**1\.** *go about, to and fro* (i.e. go about one's affairs, carry on one's business) *in,* c. acc. אֶרֶץ [Gn 42:34](https://ref.ly/Gen42:34) (E), [34:10](https://ref.ly/Gen34:10), [34:21](https://ref.ly/Gen34:21) (P); c. אֶל־אֶרֶץ [Je 14:18](https://ref.ly/Jer14:18), but dub., LXX Gf Hi Or CoHpt RothstKau *journey unto a land which* they have not known. **2\.** *Pt.* \= subst. *a trafficker, trader* (who *goes about* with wares, etc.) [Gn 23:16](https://ref.ly/Gen23:16) (P), [37:28](https://ref.ly/Gen37:28) (E), [Is 23:2](https://ref.ly/Isa23:2), [23:8](https://ref.ly/Isa23:8), [47:15](https://ref.ly/Isa47:15), [1 K 10:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:28) \= [2 Ch 1:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:16), Ez [27:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:21), [27:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:21), [27:36](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:36), [38:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek38:13); \+ תָּרִים [2 Ch 9:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:14); f., of a city or country, Ez [27:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:12), [27:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:16), [27:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:18); also in v:[15](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:15) rd. סֹחֲרֹתַיִח for MT (ידך) סְחֹרַת Sm Co Berthol Toy. **Pilp.** *Pf.* 3 ms. לִבִּי סְחַרְחַר [Psalm 38:11](https://ref.ly/Ps38:11) (Ges§ 55 e) *my heart palpittes* (\+ עֲזָבַנִי כֹחִי).

695:a. \[סָחַר], 1\.3: add ‘Targum סְחַר, אֵסְחַר *go about,* סְחוֺר \= Heb. סָבִיב.’

> \[Str5504–05] † \[סַ֫חַר] **n.m.**:[Pr 3:14](https://ref.ly/Prov3:14) traffic, gain;—cstr. סְחַר [Is 23:3](https://ref.ly/Isa23:3) \+ 2 t.; sf. סַחְרָהּ [Pr 3:14](https://ref.ly/Prov3:14) \+ 3 t.;—*traffic,* i.e. *gain* from traffic, [Is 23:3](https://ref.ly/Isa23:3), [23:18](https://ref.ly/Isa23:18) (\+ אֶתְנַנָּהּ), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Isa23:18), [45:14](https://ref.ly/Isa45:14), [Pr 3:14](https://ref.ly/Prov3:14), [31:18](https://ref.ly/Prov31:18).

> \[Str5506] † \[סְחֹרָה] **n.f.** merchandise;—cstr. סָחֹרַת Ez [27:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:15), but v. סחר *Pt.*

> \[Str5507] † סֹחֵרָה **n.f.** buckler;—[Psalm 91:4](https://ref.ly/Ps91:4) (\+ צִנָּה; fig. of ´י's faithfulness).

> \[Str4536] † \[מִסְחָר] **n.m.** appar. merchandise;—cstr. מִסְחַר הָרֹכְלִיס [1 K 10:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:15), but text prob. crpt.; Klo prop. מִסַּחַר, so BuhlLex 13; ‖ [2 Ch 9:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr9:14) has הַסֹּחֲרִים, and so Benz here (for both wds. of MT).

> \[Str5508] † \[סֹחֶ֫רֶת] **n.f.** a stone used (with marble) in paving (cf. As. *siḫru*, a precious stone, DlHWB 495\);—סָֹ\+חרֶת [Est 1:6](https://ref.ly/Esth1:6).

> \[סֵט], סֵטִים v. שֵט sub שוט. סִיג v. סוג.

> \[Str5510] † סִיוָן **n.pr.** of 3rd month, Siwan \= May–June (loan\-word from As.\-Bab. *Simânu*, cf. SchrCOT [Ne 1:1](https://ref.ly/Neh1:1) Muss\-ArnoltJRL xi (1892\), 82 ff.; Palm. סיון Lzb328 Cook84);—´בַּחֹדֶשׁ הַשְּׁלִישֹׁי הוּא־תֹדֶשׁ ס [Est 8:9](https://ref.ly/Esth8:9).

> \[Str5511] סִיחוֺן, סִיחֹן **n.pr.m. Sihon** (on format. cf. LagBN 198\);—סִיחוֺן [Nu 21:27](https://ref.ly/Num21:27) \+ 18 t., סִיחֹן v:[21](https://ref.ly/Num21:21) \+ 17 t.;—king of Amorites, [Nu 21:21](https://ref.ly/Num21:21) \+ 7 t. Nu 21 (JE), [32:33](https://ref.ly/Num32:33) (R) [Dt 1:4](https://ref.ly/Deut1:4) \+ 10 t. Dt, Ju [2:10](https://ref.ly/Judg2:10) \+ 4 t. Ju (D) \+ [13:21](https://ref.ly/Judg13:21), [13:21](https://ref.ly/Judg13:21), 13:27 (P), [11:19](https://ref.ly/Judg11:19), [11:20](https://ref.ly/Judg11:20), [11:20](https://ref.ly/Judg11:20), [11:21](https://ref.ly/Judg11:21), [Je 48:45](https://ref.ly/Jer48:45), [1 K 4:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:19), [Ne 9:22](https://ref.ly/Neh9:22), [Psalm 135:11](https://ref.ly/Ps135:11), [136:19](https://ref.ly/Ps136:19). LXX Σηων, LXXL Σιων.

> \[Str5512] † I. סִין **n.pr.loc. Sin**, i.e. *Pelusium*, E. frontier city of Egypt (Egypt. '*Imt \= clay*, of which סין is transl. (cf. Aram. סְיָן *clay*), acc. to SteindorffBAS i. 599, who cp. II ηλούσιον (πηλός \= *dirt, mud*), cf. BrugschDict. Gêogr. 108l ff.);—Ez [30:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:15) (LXX Σαιν acc.), \+ v:[16](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:16), but here Co Toy rd. plausibly סון, i.e. סְוָן \= Syene (v. \[סְוָן], סְוֵנֵה), LXX συηνη.—On Pelusium v. BDEgypt. 4 (1898\), 169\.

> \[Str5512] II. סִין **n.pr.loc.** wilderness between Elim and Sinai, מִדְבַּר־סִין, acc. to P, [Ex 16:1](https://ref.ly/Exod16:1), [17:1](https://ref.ly/Exod17:1), [Nu 33:11](https://ref.ly/Num33:11), [3:12](https://ref.ly/Num3:12); LXX Σειν, A LXXL Σιν; cf. EbGS 2\. 155 ff. and esp. DIEx 16, 1\.

> \[Str5514] † סִינַי **n. pr. mont.** Sinai;—name of mt. of law\-giving in J and esp. P (חֹרֵב in E and esp. D): Ju [5:5](https://ref.ly/Judg5:5), [Psalm 68:9](https://ref.ly/Ps68:9), [Dt 33:2](https://ref.ly/Deut33:2) (poem), also [Ex 16:1](https://ref.ly/Exod16:1) (P); usu. הַר־סִינַי (־סִיָ\+ני [Lv 7:38](https://ref.ly/Lev7:38) \+), rarely J, [Ex 19:20](https://ref.ly/Exod19:20), [19:23](https://ref.ly/Exod19:23) and perh. [34:2](https://ref.ly/Exod34:2), [34:4](https://ref.ly/Exod34:4), elsewh. mostly P, 24:16, [31:18](https://ref.ly/Exod31:18), [34:29](https://ref.ly/Exod34:29), [34:32](https://ref.ly/Exod34:32), [Lv 7:38](https://ref.ly/Lev7:38), [25:1](https://ref.ly/Lev25:1), [27:34](https://ref.ly/Lev27:34) and [26:46](https://ref.ly/Lev26:46) (H), [Nu 3:1](https://ref.ly/Num3:1), [28:6](https://ref.ly/Num28:6), also [Ne 9:13](https://ref.ly/Neh9:13); מִרְבַּר סִינַי (סִיָ\+ני [Nu 1:19](https://ref.ly/Num1:19) \+), [Ex 19:1](https://ref.ly/Exod19:1), [19:2](https://ref.ly/Exod19:2), [Lv 7:38](https://ref.ly/Lev7:38), [Nu 1:1](https://ref.ly/Num1:1), [1:19](https://ref.ly/Num1:19), [3:4](https://ref.ly/Num3:4), [3:14](https://ref.ly/Num3:14), [9:1](https://ref.ly/Num9:1), [9:5](https://ref.ly/Num9:5), [10:12](https://ref.ly/Num10:12), [26:64](https://ref.ly/Num26:64), [33:15](https://ref.ly/Num33:15), [33:16](https://ref.ly/Num33:16) (all P);—in view of these facts, and of E's use of חֹרֵב (q. v.), it is prob. that [Ex 19:11](https://ref.ly/Exod19:11), [19:18](https://ref.ly/Exod19:18) (in E passages) are from R.—LXX Σ(ε)ινα. Identif. dub.; local tradition names *Jebel Musa*, mt. at S. end of peninsular between the two arms of Red Sea, so RobBR i. 90 ff., esp. 119–122 cf. StanleySinai and Pal. 42 f. al., specif. its N.spur, *Ras Ṣafṣâfeh;* but LepsiusBriefe 345 ff., 416 ff. EbGS esp. 392 ff. al. advocate *Serbal*, NW. from *Jebel Musa* (on trad., cf Ebib. 413 ff.); v. discussion Di:Ex 19, 1\. Against both is the working of Egypt. mines in the penins. as late as the 19th dyn. (v. EbGS 135, 148 ff., 159, etc.), involving presence of soldiers there. This, and connex. with Midian (cf. also Ju [5:5](https://ref.ly/Judg5:5)), have led to search for Sinai further NE, near head of Gulf of Aḳaba, or even in Seir, so SayMonuments 263 ff., cf. GFMJu 5:5\. 6:1 and *Yakut*, cited by Id179 n. §, also v. GallAltisr. Kultst. 12; on sanctity of mt. v. further RS Sem i. 110 f., 2nd ed. 177 f. SmRel. Gesch. 30\.

> \[Str5513] † סִינִי **adj. gent.** only c. art. \= subst. [Gn 10:17](https://ref.ly/Gen10:17) \= [1 Ch 1:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:15) a Canaanite people, in north, LXX τὸν  Ασενναῖον; cf. city *Siânu*, ‘ on shore of sea, ’ in As. inscr. DlPa 282, Σιννᾶν (acc.) in possession of those holding Lebanon Straboxvi. 2\. 8 (v. DIGn).

> \[Str5515] † סִינִים **adj. gent. pl.** \= subst.; ´אֶרֶץ ס [Is 49:12](https://ref.ly/Isa49:12), identif. with *Chinese* by Thes948–950 De CheComm. al.; but see v. RichthofenChina i. 436 f., 504, rev. by YuleAcad. xiii. 339; Di Du; T. de LacouperieBOR i. 45 ff., 183 ff., who thinks of *Šina*, at foot of Hindu Kush, but unlikely; rd. prob. סְוֵנִים (or סְוָנִים), so CheIntr. Is. 275, and Hpt., cf. already JDMich.

> סִיס [Je 8:7](https://ref.ly/Jer8:7) Qr v. I. סוּס

## I סָכַךְ (id: BDB06540)

\[Str5526] † I. \[סָכַךְ] **vb.** overshadow, screen, cover (‖ √ to I. שכך (which is prob. orig., cf. Ar. شَكَّ, and Frä90); NH הֵסֵיח *cover*, and perh. סִכֵּךְ *roof over*);—**Qal** *Pf.* only 2 ms. סַכֹּ֫תָ [Ex 40:3](https://ref.ly/Exod40:3), סַכֹּ֫תָה [La 3:43](https://ref.ly/Lam3:43), [3:44](https://ref.ly/Lam3:44), סַכּ֫וֺתָה [Psalm 140:8](https://ref.ly/Ps140:8); *Impf.* 3 mpl. וַיָּסֹ֫כּוּ [1 K 8:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:7), sf. יְסֻכֻּ֫הוּ [Jb 40:22](https://ref.ly/Job40:22); *Pt. act.* סוֺכֵךְ Ez [28:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:14), [28:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:16), pl. סֹכֲבִים [Ex 25:20](https://ref.ly/Exod25:20) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** *screen, cover*, usu. co. עַ־ל of thing covered, [1 K 8:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:7), [1 Ch 28:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:18); \+ acc. of covering [Ex 40:3](https://ref.ly/Exod40:3) (P); \+ בְּ of covering [25:20](https://ref.ly/Exod25:20), [37:9](https://ref.ly/Exod37:9) (both P); c. לְ of thing covered [Psalm 140:8](https://ref.ly/Ps140:8); so (\+ בְּ instr.) בֶּעָנָן לָךְ ´ס [La 3:44](https://ref.ly/Lam3:44) *thou hast screened thyself with the clouds;* c. acc. [Jb 40:22](https://ref.ly/Job40:22) *lotus\-trees, as its shade, screen it;* abs. Ez [28:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:14), [28:16](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:16) (difficult, text prob. corrupt, Co Toy del. as gloss). **2\.** reflex. *cover oneself* בָאַף ´ס [La 3:43](https://ref.ly/Lam3:43) *thou hast covered* (clothed, panoplied) *thyself with anger* (si vera l.; cf. v:[44](https://ref.ly/Lam3:44) *supr.*.). **Hiph.** *Impf.*. 3 ms. יָ֫סֶךְ לָךְ [Psalm 91:4](https://ref.ly/Ps91:4), וַיָּ֫סֶךְ [Ex 40:1](https://ref.ly/Exod40:1) \+ 2 t.; 2 ms. תָּסֵךְ [Psalm 5:12](https://ref.ly/Ps5:12); *Inf. cstr.*. הָסֵךְ [1 S 24:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:4); *Pt*. מֵסִיךְ Ju [3:24](https://ref.ly/Judg3:24) (Ges§ 67 v);—*screen, cover*, **1\.** specif. c. אֶת־רַגְלָיו, i.e. with long garments, euphemism for evacuating the bowels, from posture assumed, so inf. Ju [3:24](https://ref.ly/Judg3:24), pt. [1 S 24:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:4) (cf. NH הֵסֵיח, and v. GFM, HPS). **2\.** elsewhere Impf., as **Qal** (expl. as Qal BaZMG xliii (1889\), 178\), c. עַ־ל [Ex 40:21](https://ref.ly/Exod40:21), of protection [Psalm 5:12](https://ref.ly/Ps5:12); c. לְ Pers. \+ בְּ instr. [91:4](https://ref.ly/Ps91:4) (of protection). **Pilp.** סִכְסֵךְ, denom., v. sub **IV.** שבך. p. 967–68

> \[Str4539] † מָסָךְ **n.\[m.]** covering, screen;—abs. ´מ [2 S 17:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:19) \+ 11 t.; cstr. מָסַח [Is 22:8](https://ref.ly/Isa22:8) \+ 12 t.;—**1\.** *covering*, large cloth spread (פרש) over well to hide persons within [2 S 17:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:19); fig. of protection, or of eye\-screen, יְהוּדָה ´וַיְגַל אֶת־מ [Is 22:8](https://ref.ly/Isa22:8); *screen*, of cloud [Psalm 105:39](https://ref.ly/Ps105:39) (פרש). **2\.** name given (in P) to each of three *screens*, of tabern.: **a.** at gate (שַׁעַר) of court [Ex 27:16](https://ref.ly/Exod27:16), [35:17](https://ref.ly/Exod35:17), [38:18](https://ref.ly/Exod38:18), [39:40](https://ref.ly/Exod39:40), [40:8](https://ref.ly/Exod40:8), [40:33](https://ref.ly/Exod40:33), [Nu 3:26](https://ref.ly/Num3:26) (פֶּתַח), [4:26](https://ref.ly/Num4:26). **b.** at entrance (פֶּתַח) of tent [Ex 26:36](https://ref.ly/Exod26:36), [26:37](https://ref.ly/Exod26:37), [35:15](https://ref.ly/Exod35:15), [36:37](https://ref.ly/Exod36:37), [39:38](https://ref.ly/Exod39:38), [40:5](https://ref.ly/Exod40:5), [40:28](https://ref.ly/Exod40:28), [Nu 3:25](https://ref.ly/Num3:25), [3:31](https://ref.ly/Num3:31), [4:25](https://ref.ly/Num4:25), **c.** ´פָּרֹכֶת הַמּ (v. ´פ), dividing off the Most Holy Place within the tent, [Ex 35:12](https://ref.ly/Exod35:12), [39:34](https://ref.ly/Exod39:34), [40:21](https://ref.ly/Exod40:21), [Nu 4:5](https://ref.ly/Num4:5).

> \[Str4540] † \[מְסֻכַָה] **n.f.** that with which one is covered, covering;—sf. מְסֻכַָתְָ Ez [28:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:13) (so Baer Ginsb; van d. H. ´מְסֻכּ) *all precious stones* were *thy covering* (\= *thou wast covered* with them);—the tradition is strong in favour of *Raphē*, but sense favours deriv. from סכך.

> \[Str4329] † \[מוּסָךְ] **n.m.** architect. term (si vera l.) of some *covered* structure, otherwise unknown;—only cstr. מוּסַח־הַשַּׁכָּת [2 K 16:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:18) Qr (Kt מיסך i.e. מֵיסַךְ);—LXX τὸν θεμέλιον τν͂ς καθέδρας, i.e. מוּסַד הַשֶּׁבֶת ?

## II סָכַךְ (id: BDB06544)

\[Str5526] †II. \[סָכַךְ] **vb.** weave together (‖ form of II. שָבַךְ; NH. Hiph. הֵסֵיח *weave*, סוּבָּה, *booth*,);—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 ms. sf. תְּסֻכֵּנִי [Psalm 139:13](https://ref.ly/Ps139:13) *thou didst weave me together* in my mother's womb. p. 968

697:b. סֹכֵךְ: transfer to I. \[סָכַךְ].

> \[Str5520] † \[סֹךְ] **n.\[m.] thicket, covert, lair**;—only sf. סֻכּוֺ [Je 25:38](https://ref.ly/Jer25:38) *lair* of ´י, under fig. of lion (but Gie סֻבְּכוֺ cf. [4:7](https://ref.ly/Jer4:7)), סֻכֹּה [Psalm 10:9](https://ref.ly/Ps10:9) of lion (sim. of wicked; Bae סֻכָּה; Lag Che We סֻבְּכוֺ); סוּכּוֺ [76:3](https://ref.ly/Ps76:3) *his covert* (of ´י under fig. of lion; ‖מְעוֺנָתוֺ); for סֻכֹּה *in his covert* [Psalm 27:5](https://ref.ly/Ps27:5) Qr rd. perh., with Kt סֻכָּה *a booth* (Ol Hup\-Now Bae cf. [31:21](https://ref.ly/Ps31:21)).

> \[Str5521] † סֻכָּה **n.f.** thicket, booth (prop. of *inter\-woven* boughs cf. [Ne 8:15](https://ref.ly/Neh8:15));—abs. ´ס [Is 1:8](https://ref.ly/Isa1:8) \+; cstr. סֻכָּת [Am 9:11](https://ref.ly/Amos9:11); sf. סֻכָּתוֺ [Jb 36:29](https://ref.ly/Job36:29), [Psalm 18:12](https://ref.ly/Ps18:12), and so rd. ‖ [2 S 22:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:12) (for MT סֻכּוֺת; De Hup\-Now HPS); usu. pl. סֻכֹּת [Gn 33:17](https://ref.ly/Gen33:17) \+; סֻכּוֺת [2 S 11:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:11) \+;—**1\.** *thicket*, lurking\-place of lions [Jb 38:40](https://ref.ly/Job38:40) (cf. \[סֹח] **1\.**). **2\.** *booth*, rude or temporary shelter, for cattle [Gn 33:17](https://ref.ly/Gen33:17) (J; distinct fr. בַּיִת), but also for warriors in the field [2 S 11:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:11), [1 K 20:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:12), [20:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:16); for watchers in vineyards [Is 1:8](https://ref.ly/Isa1:8) (sim.) [Jb 27:18](https://ref.ly/Job27:18) (sim. of frailty), for man's shelter from sun [Jon 4:5](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:5), cf. (fig.) [Is 4:6](https://ref.ly/Isa4:6) [Psalm 31:21](https://ref.ly/Ps31:21), prob. also [27:5](https://ref.ly/Ps27:5) (v. \[סֹח]); poet. of fallen house (dynasty) of David [Am 9:11](https://ref.ly/Amos9:11); of clouds as (temporary) enclosure (AV ‘ pavilion ’) of ´י in storm [Psalm 18:12](https://ref.ly/Ps18:12) \= [2 S 22:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:12), [Jb 36:29](https://ref.ly/Job36:29). **3\.** specif. of *booths*, made of boughs, in which people lived at harvest\-feast [Lv 23:42](https://ref.ly/Lev23:42), [23:42](https://ref.ly/Lev23:42), [23:43](https://ref.ly/Lev23:43) (H), [Ne 8:14](https://ref.ly/Neh8:14), [8:15](https://ref.ly/Neh8:15), [8:16](https://ref.ly/Neh8:16), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Neh8:17), [8:17](https://ref.ly/Neh8:17), hence called חַג הַסֻכּוֺת [Dt 16:13](https://ref.ly/Deut16:13), [16:16](https://ref.ly/Deut16:16), [31:10](https://ref.ly/Deut31:10), [Lv 23:34](https://ref.ly/Lev23:34) (P), [Zc 14:16](https://ref.ly/Zech14:16), [14:18](https://ref.ly/Zech14:18), [14:19](https://ref.ly/Zech14:19), [2 Ch 8:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:13), [Ezr 3:4](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:4).

## סָכַל (id: BDB06551)

\[Str5528] † \[סָכַל] **vb.** be foolish, or a fool, usu. in moral or spititual sense (Syr. ܣܟܰܠ Aph. *act foolishly;* ܣܰܟܠܴܐ *foolish*, etc., Targum סְכַל der. species, *act foolishly;* סַכְלָא *fool;* Chr Pal Aram. سكل, Aph. \= ἁμαρτάνω, also deriv. SchwallyIdlot. 62; As. *saklu*, perh. *foolish*, DlHWB 498; Aram. ܣܟܠ סְכַל *know, be intelligent, cause to understand*, etc. (der. species), cf. שכל; MeChrest. Targ. derives both these opp. mngs. fr. Ar. ܫܟܠܢ *form, likeness*. Gerber178 thinks Heb. vb. denom.);—**Piel** *Impf.* יְסַכֵּל [Is 44:25](https://ref.ly/Isa44:25), *Imv.* סַכֶּל־נָא [2 S 15:31](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:31), *make foolish, turn into foolishness*. **Niph.** *Pf.* 2 ms. נִסְכַּלְתָּ [2 Ch 16:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:9); נִסְכָּלְתָּ [1 S 13:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam13:13); 1 S. נִסְכַּלְתִּי [2 S 24:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:10) \= [1 Ch 21:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:8), *act or do foolishly*. **Hiph.** *Pf.* 2 ms. הִסְכַּלְתָּ [Gn 31:28](https://ref.ly/Gen31:28) (E), 1 S. הִסְכַּלְתִּי [1 S 26:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:21), *do foolishly, play the fool*.

> \[Str5530] † סָכָל **n.m.** fool (on format. cf. LagBN 48\):—[Je 5:21](https://ref.ly/Jer5:21), [Ec 2:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:19), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:17), [10:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:3), [10:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:3), [10:14](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:14), pl. adj. סְכָלִים [Je 4:22](https://ref.ly/Jer4:22).

> \[Str5529] † סֶ֫כֶל **n.m.** folly;—[Ec 10:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:6).

> \[Str5531] † סִכְלוּת, שִכְלוּת **n.f.** folly;—´ס [Ec 2:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:3), [2:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:12), [2:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:13), [7:25](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:25), [10:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:1), [10:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:13); ´שׁ [Ec 1:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:17) (ש erroneously for ס).

* **Associated Passages:** Ecclesiastes 10:1; Ecclesiastes 1:17; Isaiah 44:25; Jeremiah 4:22; Jeremiah 5:21; 1 Samuel 13:13; Ecclesiastes 10:14; 2 Chronicles 16:9; Ecclesiastes 10:13; 1 Samuel 26:21; 2 Samuel 15:31; Genesis 31:28; Ecclesiastes 10:3; Ecclesiastes 2:3; 1 Chronicles 21:8; Ecclesiastes 10:6; Ecclesiastes 7:25; Ecclesiastes 7:17; Ecclesiastes 2:19; Ecclesiastes 2:12; 2 Samuel 24:10; Ecclesiastes 2:13


## I סָכַן (id: BDB06555)

\[Str5532] † I. \[סָכַן] **vb.** be of use of service, benefit (Tel Am. *sakânu, care for* (c. prep. *ana*) ZimZA vi. 248 WklTA Gloss.; Ph. סכן *prefect* Lzb329; cf. perh. Tel Am. *zukini* as loan\-word Wkll. c.);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. only Jb.: יִסְכֹּן [22:2](https://ref.ly/Job22:2), וֺן\- [15:3](https://ref.ly/Job15:3), יִסְכָּ־ן [22:2](https://ref.ly/Job22:2) \+ 2 t.; *Pt.* סֹכֵן [Is 22:15](https://ref.ly/Isa22:15); f. סֹכֶ֫נֶת [1 K 1:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:2), [1:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:4); —**1\.** *be of use* or *service*, only pt. ´וּתְהִי לוֺ ס [1 K 1:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:2) *and let her become servitress to him,* so v:[4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:4) (\+ וְתְּשָׁרֲתֵהוּ); סֹכֵן [Is 22:15](https://ref.ly/Isa22:15) \= *servitor, steward.* **2\.** *benefit, profit*, abs. [Jb 15:3](https://ref.ly/Job15:3); c. לְ Pers. [22:2](https://ref.ly/Job22:2), [35:3](https://ref.ly/Job35:3), עַ־ל Pers. [22:2](https://ref.ly/Job22:2); c. acc. [34:9](https://ref.ly/Job34:9) (or abs. *gain benefit,* גבר subj.). **Hiph.** *Pf.* 1 S. הִסְכַּ֫נְתִּי [Nu 22:20](https://ref.ly/Num22:20); 2 ms. הִסְבַָּנְתָּה [Psalm 139:3](https://ref.ly/Ps139:3); *Imv.* הַסְכֶּן־נָא [Jb 22:21](https://ref.ly/Job22:21); *Inf. abs*. הַסְכֵּן [Nu 22:30](https://ref.ly/Num22:30); —*be used, wont,* strictly *exhibit use*, or *habit*, הַהַסְכֵּן הִסְכַּנְתִּי לַעֲשוֺת לְ כֹּה [Nu 22:30](https://ref.ly/Num22:30) (J) *have I ever shewn the habit of doing thus to thee?* hence *shew harmony with* (עִם) one [Jb 22:21](https://ref.ly/Job22:21), *be familiar with, know intimately* (subj. ´י) [Psalm 139:3](https://ref.ly/Ps139:3) (c. acc. דְּרָכַי).

> \[Str4543] † מִסְכְּנוֺת **n.f.pl. supply, storage** (DlPr 186\);—´מ(חַ) עָרֵי [Ex 1:11](https://ref.ly/Exod1:11) (J), [1 K 9:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs9:19) \= [2 Ch 8:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:6), [2 Ch 8:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr8:4), [17:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:12); ´ע ´מ [16:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:4) prob. corrupt (Be al.; v. עִיר); ´מ alone [32:28](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:28) *storage\-places, magazines*.

> \[Str5548] † סַלְכָה **n. pr. loc.** city on E. border of Bashan, [Dt 3:10](https://ref.ly/Deut3:10), [Jos 12:5](https://ref.ly/Josh12:5), [13:11](https://ref.ly/Josh13:11) (all D), [1 Ch 5:11](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:11); \= Nab. עלתד (Lzb358 Cook101), mod. *Ṣalḫad, Ṣarḫad*, on S. spur of *Jebel Hauran*, c. 63 m. due E. of Jordan; cf. BuhlGeogr. 252 Dr:[Dt 3:10](https://ref.ly/Deut3:10); LXX Σελχα, Ελχα, etc. (Σ lost often after ἔως).

## I סָלַל (id: BDB06580)

\[Str5549] †I. \[סָלַל] **vb.** lift up, cast up (NH סִלְסֵל *esteem highly*, סִלְסוּל *loftiness, distinction;* סוּלָּם ladder; Targum סוּלְּמָא *id.;* MI26 מסלת *highway;* Ph. סלמת *stair (?)* Lzb329; As. *sellu, sillu*, perh. *breast\-works* DlHWB 501; Ar. سَلّ is *draw out, forth* Lane1395 \= שָׁלַל, but سُلَّمٌ ladder (a loan\-wd. acc. to SchwallyZMG liii (1899\), 197\), cf. סֻלָּם; v. also *ṭrry, siege\-wall*, Egypt. loan\-word from סֹלְלָה acc. to WMMAs. u. Eur. 101\);—**Qal** *Impf.* וַיָּסֹ֫לּוּ [Jb 19:12](https://ref.ly/Job19:12), [30:12](https://ref.ly/Job30:12); *Imv.* mpl. סֹ֫לּוּ [Is 57:14](https://ref.ly/Isa57:14) \+ 4 t.; sf. סָלּוּהָ [Je 50:26](https://ref.ly/Jer50:26) (cf. סֶ֫לָה); *Pt. pass.* סְלוּלָה [Je 18:15](https://ref.ly/Jer18:15); סְלֻלָה [Pr 15:19](https://ref.ly/Prov15:19); —**1\.** *cast up a highway:* מְסִלָּה ´ס [Is 62:10](https://ref.ly/Isa62:10), [62:10](https://ref.ly/Isa62:10), without obj. [Is 57:14](https://ref.ly/Isa57:14), [57:14](https://ref.ly/Isa57:14), דֶּרֶח לאֹ סְלוּלָה [Je 18:15](https://ref.ly/Jer18:15); fig. of path of upright [Pr 15:19](https://ref.ly/Prov15:19). **2\.** *cast up a way:* דֶּרֶח c. על *against*, [Jb 19:12](https://ref.ly/Job19:12) (of besieger, in fig.), ארה [Jb 30:12](https://ref.ly/Job30:12) (of besetting foe); Bab. as a heap of garbage [Je 50:26](https://ref.ly/Jer50:26). **3\.** *lift up* (a song) [Psalm 68:5](https://ref.ly/Ps68:5), c. לְ Pers. (‖שׁיר, זמּר; most as **1\.**). **Pilp.** *Imv.* sf. סַלְסְלֶהָ [Pr 4:8](https://ref.ly/Prov4:8) *exalt her* (i.e. Wisdom), i.e. (cf. Toy) *esteem highly, prize.* **Hithp.** *Pt.* מִסְתּוֺלֵל [Ex 9:17](https://ref.ly/Exod9:17) (J) *exalt oneself*, c. בְּ *against* (denom. from סֹלְלָה acc. to Gerber52); cf. [Ecclus 39:24](https://ref.ly/Sir39:24), [40:28](https://ref.ly/Sir40:28).

> \[Str5542] † סֶָלָה **vb.** lift up (voices in ברוך לעולם), or exalt (´לעולם י) (*Imv.* of סָלַל (cf. [Pr 4:8](https://ref.ly/Prov4:8), [Psalm 68:5](https://ref.ly/Ps68:5); חִגָּיוֺן סלה [9:17](https://ref.ly/Ps9:17), v. ´הִגּ), properly סָֹלָּה, סֶָלֶה poss. Qr \= נֶצַה, Hexapla σελ but cf. הֶָרָה Ew554 Köii. 1, 539; LXX Sym Theod διάΨαλμα, expl. Suidas μέλους ἐναλλαγή; Theodoret Hippolytus μέλους μεταβολή (cf. Hexapla Hb [3:3](https://ref.ly/Hab3:3) μεταβολὴ διαΨάλματος; Syr. Hexapla Aq. עוניתא in 5 PsalmPsalm, Field in [Psalm 38:12](https://ref.ly/Ps38:12)); LXX adds διάΨαλμα e.g. [2:2](https://ref.ly/Ps2:2), [34:11](https://ref.ly/Ps34:11), [94:15](https://ref.ly/Ps94:15), after final editing of Heb. Psalter ; so Psalms of [Sol. 17:31](https://ref.ly/Ps17:31), [18:10](https://ref.ly/Ps18:10) on same principles as in MT; used therefore with full knowledge that it indicated some kind of interruption or change in the regular rendering. סלה is used in שמונה עשרה after Benedictions 3, 18, and after other early Jewish prayers, shewing knowledge, c. 100 A. D. ; Aq. gives ἀεί (Theod. also [Psalm 9:17](https://ref.ly/Ps9:17)) ; Sexta διαπαντός (except [20:4](https://ref.ly/Ps20:4) εἰς τέλος); Quinta εἰς τοὺς αἰῶνας Jer. *semper*, Targum usu. לעלמא, לעלמין; but [39:6](https://ref.ly/Ps39:6) לחיי עלמא, [44:9](https://ref.ly/Ps44:9) לעלמי עלמין, [48:9](https://ref.ly/Ps48:9) עד עלמי עלמין, [49:14](https://ref.ly/Ps49:14) לעלמא דאתי; uniform tradition best expl. by closing contents of the Benedictions, מהעולם ועד העולם. So Jer. classes *sela* with *amen* and *salom;* and Jacob of Edessa in Bar [Hebr. 10:1](https://ref.ly/Heb10:1) cp. Christian *Amen* of the people after *Gloria*);—this interpr. agrees with usage: 71 t. in 39 PsalmPsalm, 3 t. LXX), [9:21](https://ref.ly/Ps9:20) (LXX combines 9 and 10\); elsewh. at close of strophe, [3:3](https://ref.ly/Ps3:3), [3:5](https://ref.ly/Ps3:5), [4:3](https://ref.ly/Ps4:3), [4:5](https://ref.ly/Ps4:5), [7:6](https://ref.ly/Ps7:6), [9:17](https://ref.ly/Ps9:17), [24:6](https://ref.ly/Ps24:6), [32:4](https://ref.ly/Ps32:4), [32:5](https://ref.ly/Ps32:5), [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ps32:7), [39:6](https://ref.ly/Ps39:6), [39:12](https://ref.ly/Ps39:12), [46:4](https://ref.ly/Ps46:4), [46:8](https://ref.ly/Ps46:8), [47:5](https://ref.ly/Ps47:5), [48:9](https://ref.ly/Ps48:9), [49:13](https://ref.ly/Ps49:13) (so rd. These editors found it in earlier Psalters. Davidic Psalter uses 20 of them, so few in proportion that it is not characteristic of this Psalter; but Korahite 9 (out of 12\), and Asaph 7 \+ [80:8](https://ref.ly/Ps80:8) \[LXX] (prob. 8 out of 12\); appar. it came into use in time of these editors. In Diretor's Psalter musical terms are added to 19 of the 28 it uses (and only to ten others, of which some could hardly use סלה); PsalmPsalm with סלה all (except 61, 81\) name the kind of Psalm in title: 3 מִכְתָּם, 7 מַשְכִּיל, 10 שִׁיר, 26 (27\) others מִזְמוֺר, Hb 3 תְּפִלָּה; ´ס is esp. frequent with שִׁיר and מַשְכִּיל, terms associated with musical rendering. It prob. came into use in late Persian period in connexion with PsalmPsalm used with musical accompaniment in public worship, to indicate place of benedictions. It was not added by later editors to other psalms; but was revived in first century B.C., and continued in use for some time (v. Jacob: ZAW xvi (1896\), 129 f. BrJBL 1809 EGBriggsAM. J. Sem. Lang. Oct. 1899, 1 ff.).

## II סלל (id: BDB06586)

II. סלל (√ of foll.; cf. NH סִלְסֵל *plait, curl* hair; NH סַל Targum סַלָּא, Talm. סִילְתָּא, Syr. ܣܰܠܴܐ Chr Pal Aram. ܣܠܐ, all \= *basket;* Ar. سَلٌّ, سَلَّةٌ prob. loan\-wd. Frä75 f.; cf. SchwallyIdiot. 63\).

700:b. II. סלל, 1\. 3: add ‘cf. *sull*, a round wicker basket used by women in Palestine for carrying fruit on their heads to market, BaldenspergerPEF 1904, 135\.’

## סלע (id: BDB06589)

סלע (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. سَلَعَ *cleave, split,* سِلْعٌ *cleft, fissure*, Lane1406, hence סֶלַע *split, jagged cliff, crag*, oft. *isolated* (split off) *rock* (cf. WetzstDe Is 3, 696–707; and esp. ZAW iii (1883\), 273\); then of smaller fragments, NH סֶלַע *rock, stone*, also *scale* (of serpent), *weight* (in trade); Aram. סִלְעָא specif. *weight* for coin; Nab. סלי, a coin Lzb329 Cook85).

## סָלַף (id: BDB06593)

\[Str5557] סָלַף **vb.** twist, pervert, overturn (Targum סְלַף *twist* (rare); Ar. سَلَفَ is *pass, pass away, come to naught* Lane1407 f.);—**Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יְסַלֵּף [Jb 12:19](https://ref.ly/Job12:19) \+ 2 t., וַיְסַלֵּף [Pr 22:12](https://ref.ly/Prov22:12); 3 fs. תְּסַלֵּף [Pr 13:6](https://ref.ly/Prov13:6), [19:3](https://ref.ly/Prov19:3); *Pt.* מְסַלֵּף [Pr 21:12](https://ref.ly/Prov21:12); —**1\.** *pervert*, [Ex 23:8](https://ref.ly/Exod23:8) (E) a bribe *perverteth* the case (cause, דִּבְרֵי) of righteous, \= [Dt 16:19](https://ref.ly/Deut16:19). **2\.** *subvert, turn upside down, ruin* (only WisdLt): c. acc. Pers. [Jb 12:19](https://ref.ly/Job12:19), [Pr 13:6](https://ref.ly/Prov13:6) (opp. תִּצֹּר), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Prov21:12); ´אִוֶּלֶת אָדָם תּ דַּרְכּוֺ [19:3](https://ref.ly/Prov19:3) *a man's folly subverteth his way;* וַיְסַלֵּף דִּבְרֵי בֹגֵד [22:12](https://ref.ly/Prov22:12) *and he* (´י) *subverteth the affairs of a treacherous* man.

> \[Str5558] † סֶ֫לֶף **n. m.**:[Pr 11:3](https://ref.ly/Prov11:3) Qr crookedness, crooked dealing:—´ס abs. [Pr 15:4](https://ref.ly/Prov15:4) (of tongue); cstr. בֹּגְדִים ´ס [11:3](https://ref.ly/Prov11:3).

## סמם (id: BDB06604)

סמם (√ of foll.; meaning dub.; if akin to Ar. شَمَّ *smell* Lane1593, مَشْمُومٌ any *fragrant plant* Id1594, then ס must be for ש; in that case ´ס perh. loan\-word in Heb.; NH *id., spice, drug* (Ecclus ´ש [38:4](https://ref.ly/Sir38:4) m.), Aram. סַמָּא *id.;* ܣܰܡܳܐ *drug, pigment;* Ar. سَمٌّ *poison* is appar. loan\-word Frä262).

Ex, [Lv 4:7](https://ref.ly/Lev4:7), [16:12](https://ref.ly/Lev16:12), [Nu 4:16](https://ref.ly/Num4:16), [2 Ch 2:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:3), [3:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr3:11).

> \[Str5589] † \[סְעַפָּה] **n. f.** bough, branch;—pl. sf. סְעַפֹּתָיו Ez [31:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:6), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:8) (of king under fig. of cedar).

> \[Str5586] † I. \[סֵעֵף] **vb. Pi. denom.** lop off boughs;—*Pt.* סְסָעֵף פֻּארָה [Is 10:33](https://ref.ly/Isa10:33) ´י… *shall lop off* (the) crown of branches.

> \[Str5634] † סַרְעַפָּה **n. f.** bough (with infixed ר; cf. Ges§ 85 w, as transition\-cons. Köii. 1, 472; cf. BAram., Kau§ 62 F. SelleDe Aramaismis Lib. Ezech. 17\);—pl. sf. סַרְעַפֹּתָיו Ez [31:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:5) (of Pharaoh under fig. of tree, cf. Co Bertho1 Toy).

> \[Str5588] † II. \[סֵעֵף] (Köii. 106\) **adj.** divided, half hearted;—pl. abs. as subst., in religious sense, סֵעֲפִים שָנֵאתִי [Psalm 119:113](https://ref.ly/Ps119:113) *half\-hearted ones do I hate*.

## סָפָה (id: BDB06641)

Dr), sf. לִסְפּוֺתָהּ [Psalm 40:15](https://ref.ly/Ps40:15) *to snatch it away* (i.e. my life).—וְסָפ֫וּ [Am 3:15](https://ref.ly/Amos3:15) v. סוף. **Niph.** *Pf .* 3 ms. וְנִסְפָּה consec. [1 S 26:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:10); *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּסָּפֶה [Gn 19:15](https://ref.ly/Gen19:15), [19:17](https://ref.ly/Gen19:17); 1 S. אֶסָּפֶה [1 S 27:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:1); 2 mpl. תִּסָּפוּ [Nu 16:26](https://ref.ly/Num16:26), תִּסָּפָוּ [1 S 12:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:25); *Pt.* נִסְפֶּה [Is 13:15](https://ref.ly/Isa13:15), [Pr 13:23](https://ref.ly/Prov13:23) ([1 Ch 21:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:12) v. infr.);—**1\.** *be swept away, destroyed*, [Gn 19:15](https://ref.ly/Gen19:15) (בַּעֲוֺן הָעִיר  *by reason of the iniquity of the city),* v:[17](https://ref.ly/Gen19:17) (both J), [Nu 16:26](https://ref.ly/Num16:26) (JE) [1 S 12:25](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:25), [26:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:10) (in battle), \+ בְּיַד of agent [1 S 27:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam27:1); cf. וְיֵשׁ נִסְפֶּה בְּלאֹ מִשְׁפָּט [Pr 13:23](https://ref.ly/Prov13:23) *and there is that is swept away for lack of justice.*—[1 Ch 21:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:12) rd. נוּסְ (as ‖ [2 S 24:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:13), v. נוּס). **2\.** *be caught up, captured,* [Is 13:15](https://ref.ly/Isa13:15) (‖נִמְצָא). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 1 S. אֵסְפֶּה עָלֵימוֺ רָעוֺת [Dt 32:23](https://ref.ly/Deut32:23) *I will catch up against them calamities*, but read אֹסְפָה *I will gather* (√ אסף), or \< אֹסִפָה (Di Dr BuhlLex 13\)  *I will add,* i.e.*multiply* (√ יסף; v. ## II ספח (id: BDB06643)

II. ספח (√ of foll.; appar. *pour out;* cf. Ar. سَفَحَ *pour out, shed* (blood; Qor 6:146\); NH מִפֵּחַ of river, *cast out* alluvial soil, make new land).

> \[Str5599] † I. \[סָפִיחַ] **n.\[m.]** outpouriug(?);—pl. sf. תִּשְׁטֹף־סְפִיחֶיהָ עֲפַר הָאָרֶץ [Jb 14:19](https://ref.ly/Job14:19) (si vera l.) *its outpourings sweep away the dust of the earth*, so most, but dub.; Bu prop. …סְחִיפָה, cf. מָטָר סֹחֵף [Pr 28:3](https://ref.ly/Prov28:3).

> \[Str5599] †II. סָפִיחַ **n.\[m.]** growth from spilled kernels ((kernels) *poured out*, accidentally, in harvesting, acc. to most; BuhlLex 13 al. think of *additional* growth, √ I. ספח);—it is what springs up of itself in second year, and serves as food when no grain could be sown: abs. ´ס [2 K 19:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:29) \= [Is 37:30](https://ref.ly/Isa37:30); cstr. סְפִיחַ קְצִירְ [Lv 25:5](https://ref.ly/Lev25:5); sf. תִּקְצְרוּ אֶת־סְפִיחֶיהַ v:[11](https://ref.ly/Lev25:11).

> \[Str4939] † מִשְפָּח **n.\[m.]** prob. outpouring (of blood), bloodshed (ש subst. for ס; word chosen for assonance with מִשְׁפָּט);—abs., וַיְקַו ´לְמִשְׁפָּט וְחִנֵּה מ [Is 5:7](https://ref.ly/Isa5:7) *and he waited for justice and lo! bloodshed,* so Ges Hi Ew Di Gu Du CheComm. CheHpt.(dub.).

[Jb 34:37](https://ref.ly/Job34:37) (in presumptuousness, against God). **2\.***slap, chastise*, c. acc. Pers. [Jb 34:26](https://ref.ly/Job34:26) (subj. God). **3\.** *splash* מוֺאָב בְּקִיאוֺ ´וְס [Je 48:26](https://ref.ly/Jer48:26) *and Moab shall splash* (fall with a splash) *into his vomit*, \> Thes BuhlLex 13 *empty himself (?), throw up* (cf. syr. ܣܦܩ Pa.). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 mpl. וּבְיַלְרֵי נָכְרִים יַשְפִּ֫יקוּ [Is 2:6](https://ref.ly/Isa2:6) (rd. perh. וּבִידֵי Hi Du Che, *and into the hands of foreigners they clap*, lit. *cause to clap*, sc. their hands; \> LXX SyrVer from 1\. שפק q.v., *they abound in, have a sufficiency of).* p.974

Thes); Du reads מִשְפָּק לְחֵמָה lest *chastisement* incite thee *to wrath*.—On [Jb 20:22](https://ref.ly/Job20:22) (edd.) v. שֵפֶק. p.974

Zadok [Ne 13:13](https://ref.ly/Neh13:13); —[2 K 22:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:3), [22:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:8), [22:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:9), [22:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:10), [22:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs22:12) \= [2 Ch 34:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:15), [34:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:18), [34:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:20); [1 Ch 24:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:6) (Levite); שַר הַצָּבָא ´הַסּ [2 K 25:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:19) \= [Je 52:25](https://ref.ly/Jer52:25) (si vera l. in K) implies military authority (but ´שַר הַצּ perh. gloss GFMJu 5, 14\); Ew Sta al. read (as Je) *scribe of general*. **c.** in Persia, *secretaries* who wrote out (king's) decrees, acc. to [Est 3:12](https://ref.ly/Esth3:12), [8:9](https://ref.ly/Esth8:9). **d.** [2 Ch 26:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:11) uses ´ס for muster\-officer; [1 Ch 2:55](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:55) mentions families of סוֺפְרִים; [2 Ch 34:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:13) knows ´ס as Levites; ´ס [1 Ch 27:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:32) \= *man of learning*. **2\.** professional class of *learned men, ‘ scribes ’* able to read and write [Je 36:26](https://ref.ly/Jer36:26), [36:32](https://ref.ly/Jer36:32); with writing implements, ´מָהִיר עֵט ס [Psalm 45:2](https://ref.ly/Ps45:2), ´קֶסֶת הַסּ Ez [9:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:2), [9:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:3), ´תַּעַר הַסּ [Je 36:23](https://ref.ly/Jer36:23); esp. *learned* in the law, and assuming to issue its precepts, עֵט שֶׁקֶר סֹפְרִים [8:8](https://ref.ly/Jer8:8). **3\.** specif. of Ezra, at once priest (כֹּהֵן) and learned in the law (סֹפֵר), [Ezr 7:11](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:11), ´דִּבְרֵי מִצְוֺת י ´ס v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:11) \[cf. Aram. v:[12](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:12), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:21)], מָהִיר בְּתוֺרֵת משֶׁה ´ס v:[6](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:6); [Ne 8:1](https://ref.ly/Neh8:1), [8:4](https://ref.ly/Neh8:4), [8:9](https://ref.ly/Neh8:9), [8:13](https://ref.ly/Neh8:13), [12:26](https://ref.ly/Neh12:26), [12:36](https://ref.ly/Neh12:36).

> \[Str5610] †I. סְפָר **n.\[m.]** enumeration census (late; on format. cf. LagBN 175\);—abs. ´ס, sq. אֲשֶׁר as acc. cogn. with סָפַר [2 Ch 2:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:16).

> \[Str5611] †II. \[סְפָר] **n.pr.loc.** in S. Arabia;—only c. ה loc. סְפָ֫רָה [Gn 10:30](https://ref.ly/Gen10:30), LXX Σωφηρα; usu. indentif. with *Ṭ̄afâr* (ظَفَار, pronounced إِصْفَار, إِسْفَار acc. to Thes), ancient city of Ḥimyarite kings (Ptol. Σαπφαρ μητρόπολις v. SprengerAite Geogr. Arab.:§ 301 et pass.), so Thes TuchComm. Gen. 2, 212, but dub. GlaserSkizze ii. 437, ס \= צ improb. DIad loc. LagBN 61\.

Targum Symm.); \>Du (after LXX Bab א SyrVer Vulgate) reads סְפָרוֺת (cf. סִפְרָה supr.). p. 707

> \[Str5614] † \[סְפָרֵד?], סְפָרַָד **n.pr.loc.** Ob:[20](https://ref.ly/Obad1:20) LXX Εφραθα, Qa Σαφαραδ; loc. Dub.; SpiegAPK 242 NöZMG xxxiii (1879\), 323 CheFounders 312 SayMonuments 483 al. cp. *Sparda* in Asia Minor (\= *Sardis ?* Behistuni, 15 Persep. 1:12 NRa 28\); SchrCOTad loc.(cf. KGF 116 ff.) DlPar 249, cf. GASm12 Proph. ii. 176, cp. *Šaparda* in SW. Media (time of Sargon); a *Saparda* also NE. from Nineveh (Esarhaddon's time), cf. KnudtzonAss. Gebete, Nos. 8, 11, 30\.

> \[Str5617] † סְפַרְוַ֫יִם **n.pr.loc.** city conquered by king of Assyr.;—´ס [2 K 17:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:14), [18:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:34) \= סְפַָ\+ויִם [Is 36:19](https://ref.ly/Isa36:19), עִיר סְפַרְָ\+ויִם [2 K 19:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:13) \= [Is 37:13](https://ref.ly/Isa37:13); סְפַרְָ\+ויִם also [2 K 17:31](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:31) b Qr (Kt. ספרים); LXX Σεπφαρουαιν, Επφαρουαιμ, etc., LXXL Σεπφαρειμ;—usu. identif. with *Sippara* (on a canal), between Bagdad and Babyl., a city in two parts, *Sipp.* of *Samasû* (mod. *Abbu Habba*), and *Sipp. of Anunitu(m)* on opp. side of canal, hence du. ´ס \= *the two Sipparas* SchrCOT [2 K 17:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:24), cf. DlPar 209–212 TieleGesch. 88; this recently disputed, in view of mention with Hamath and other northern cities ([2 K 17:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:24) and esp. [18:34](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:34)), v. HalZA ii. 401 ff. Di:[Is 36:19](https://ref.ly/Isa36:19) WklAlttest, Unters. 101 Benz:[2 K 17:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:24) al., who cp. *Šabara’ in* city conquered by Šalmanašarid IV (Wkl in SchrKB ii. 276\); but this by no means certain.

## סָקַל (id: BDB06679)

\[Str5619] † \[סָקַל] **vb.** stone, put to death by stoning, also (**Pi.**) free from stones (appears like denom., but noun unknown; NH סְקִילָא *execution by stoning*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 ms. sf. וּסְקַלְתּוֺ consec. [Dt 13:11](https://ref.ly/Deut13:11); 3 mpl sf. וּסְקָלֻנִי consec. [Ex 17:4](https://ref.ly/Exod17:4); 2 mpl וּסְקַלְתֶּם consec. [Dt 22:24](https://ref.ly/Deut22:24), etc.; *Impf.* 3 mpl. יִסְקְלוּ [Jos 7:25](https://ref.ly/Josh7:25), sf. יִסְקְלֻהוּ [1 K 21:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:13), etc.; *Imv.* mpl. sf. מִקְלֻהוּ [1 K 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:10); *Inf. abs.* סָקוֺל [Ex 19:13](https://ref.ly/Exod19:13), [21:28](https://ref.ly/Exod21:28); *cstr.* sf. סָקְלוֺ [1 S 30:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:6); —*pelt with stones, stone to death* (as penalty) c. acc. Pers. vel animal., [Ex 8:22](https://ref.ly/Exod8:22), [17:4](https://ref.ly/Exod17:4) (both JE) [1 S 30:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam30:6), [1 K 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:10), abs. [Ex 19:3](https://ref.ly/Exod19:3), [21:28](https://ref.ly/Exod21:28) (JE); c. acc. Pers. \+ בְּ instr. בָּאֲבָנִים [Dt 13:11](https://ref.ly/Deut13:11), [17:5](https://ref.ly/Deut17:5), [22:21](https://ref.ly/Deut22:21), [22:24](https://ref.ly/Deut22:24), [Jos 7:25](https://ref.ly/Josh7:25), [1 K 21:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:13). **Niph.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִסָּקֵל *be stoned to death* Ez [19:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek19:13), [21:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:28), [21:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:29), [21:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:32) (JE). **Pi.** *Impf.* 3 ms. יְסַקֵּל [2 S 16:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:6), [16:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:13), sf. יְסַקְּלֵהוּ [Is 5:2](https://ref.ly/Isa5:2); *Imv.* mpl. סַקְּלוּ [Is 62:10](https://ref.ly/Isa62:10); —**1\.** *stone, pelt with stones*, c. acc. Pers. \+ בָּאֲבָנִים [2 S 16:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:6), acc. om. v:[13](https://ref.ly/2Sam16:13) (‖עִפַּר בֶּעָפָר). **2\.** *free* vineyard (acc.) *from stones* (Ges§ 52 h) [Is 5:2](https://ref.ly/Isa5:2); so also [2 S 4:6](https://ref.ly/2Sam4:6), reading pt. f. מְסַקְּלָה חִטִּים lo, the portress *was cleansing what from stones* (for MT ´לֽקְּחֵי ח), so We Dr Bu Kit Löhr HPS, after LXX; (\> rdg. however mostly סֹלְקָה, **Qal** *Pt.*); סַקְּלוּ מֵאֶבֶן [Is 62:10](https://ref.ly/Isa62:10) *free* (highway) *fr. stones.* **Pu.** *Pf.* סֻקַּל *be stoned to death* [1 K 21:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:14), [21:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:15).—Not in HP; these and other writings (exc. above) use syn. רגס, cf. BrHex 73\.
