## סָרַח (id: BDB06688)

\[Str5628] † \[סָרַח **vb.** go free, be unrestrained, overrun, exceed (NH סָרוּחַ *overhanging, flapping;* Ar. سَرَح of camels, etc., *pasture where they please;* also *send forth* to pasture; II. *let* a wife *go free; let down* the hair; سُرُح *easy*.—NH סָרַח, Aram. ܣܪܰܚ usu. \= *corrupt, sin*);—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּסְרַח [Ex 26:12](https://ref.ly/Exod26:12); *Pt. act.* f. סֹרַ֫חַת Ez [17:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:6); *pass.* סָרוּחַ (cf. Köii. 1, 137 Ges§ 50 f. BaNB 180\) [Ex 26:13](https://ref.ly/Exod26:13); pl. סְרוּחִים [Am 6:7](https://ref.ly/Amos6:7), סְרֻחִים v:[4](https://ref.ly/Amos6:4); cstr. סְרוּחֵי Ez [23:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:15); —**1\.** *go free, be unrestrained:* וּסְרֻחִים עַל־עַרְשוֺתָם [Am 6:4](https://ref.ly/Amos6:4) i.e. prob. \= *and are sprawling* upon their couches (in contempt. hyperbole), ´מִרַזְח ס v:[7](https://ref.ly/Amos6:7) *the revelry of sprawlers;* pt. act. of *overrunning, spreading* vine Ez [17:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:6). **2\.** of *overhanging* stuffs (prop. *overrunning, exceeding, extended beyond limits*) תִּסְרַח עַל אֲחֹרֵי הַמִּשְׁכָּן [Ex 26:12](https://ref.ly/Exod26:12) (P) *it shall overrun* (*overhang*), over the back of the tabern., so יִהְיֶה סָרוּחַ ´עַל־צִדֵּי הַמּ v:[13](https://ref.ly/Exod26:13); סְרוּחֵי טְבוּלִים Ez [23:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:15) *overhung of turbans*, extended with respect to turbans, \= with pendant turbans (cf. \[טְבוּל]). **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 fs. נִסְרְחָה חָכְמָתָם [Je 49:7](https://ref.ly/Jer49:7) usu. *is their wisdom let loose* (i.e. *dismissed, gone*)? (‖הַאֵין עוֺד חָכְמָה בְּתֵימָן אָֽבְד עֵצָה מִבַּנִים); but NöExpos. May, 1897, 363 *is corrupt* (cf. NH, and esp. Aram.).

> \[Str5629] † סֶ֫רַח **n. m.** excess;—הָעֹדֵף ´ס [Ex 26:12](https://ref.ly/Exod26:12) (P) *the excess* (sc. of tent\-covering), *which remains over*.

> \[Str5630] † \[סִרְיֹן **n.\[m.]** armour (‖ form of שִׁרְיוֺן q. v.);—sf. יִתְעַל בְּסִרְיֹנוֺ [Je 51:3](https://ref.ly/Jer51:3) *let him raise himself in his armour;* pl. לִבְשׁוּ הַסִּרְיֹנוֺת [46:4](https://ref.ly/Jer46:4).

> \[Str5631] סָרִיס:45 **n. m.** eunuch (NH *id.*, Targum סָרִיסָא, Syr. ܣܳܪܝܣܳܐ (all c. vb. denom. \= *emasculate*), OAram. סרסא Lzb331 Cook86; Ar. سَرِسٌ, سَرِيسٌ (vb. سَرِسَ *be impotent*), Kam Frey (not Lane); prob. foreign word; acc. to JenZA vii. 174 \= As. *šarêši* (*rîši*), *he who is the head, chief*, cf. BrockLex 239 b DlHWB 694 (*ša\-riš* ?), ZimZMG liii. 1899, 116; *eunuch* being specialized meaning; in any case Ar. is Aram. loan\-word);—abs. ´ס [1 K 22:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:9) \+ 7 t.; cstr. סְרִיס [Gn 37:36](https://ref.ly/Gen37:36) \+ 3 t.; pl. סָרִיסִים [2 K 9:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:32) \+ 17 t.; cstr. סְרִיסֵי [Gn 40:7](https://ref.ly/Gen40:7) \+ 4 t.; sf. סָרִיסָיו [Gn 40:2](https://ref.ly/Gen40:2) \+ 4 t., סָרִיסֶ֫יהָ [Est 4:4](https://ref.ly/Esth4:4); —*eunuch*, of Pharaoh [Gn 37:36](https://ref.ly/Gen37:36), [40:2](https://ref.ly/Gen40:2), [40:7](https://ref.ly/Gen40:7) (all E), married [39:1](https://ref.ly/Gen39:1) (RJE); of Isr. kings [1 S 8:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:15) (predict.), [1 K 22:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:9) \= [2 Ch 18:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:8), [2 K 8:6](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:6), [9:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:32) (all of N. Isr.); [1 Ch 28:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:1) (in David's time). [2 K 23:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:11), [24:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:12), [24:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:15) cf. [Je 29:2](https://ref.ly/Jer29:2), also [34:19](https://ref.ly/Jer34:19), [38:7](https://ref.ly/Jer38:7), [41:16](https://ref.ly/Jer41:16) (all in Judah; so likewise) [2 K 25:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:19) (military officer) \= [Je 52:25](https://ref.ly/Jer52:25); of Bab. kings [2 K 20:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs20:18) \= [Is 39:7](https://ref.ly/Isa39:7) (predict.); ascribed to Nebuch. in Dn, רַב סָרִיסָיו [Dn 1:3](https://ref.ly/Dan1:3), שַר הַסָּרִיסִים v:[7](https://ref.ly/Dan1:7), v:[8](https://ref.ly/Dan1:8), v:[9](https://ref.ly/Dan1:9), v:[10](https://ref.ly/Dan1:10), v:[11](https://ref.ly/Dan1:11), v:[18](https://ref.ly/Dan1:18); to king of Persia in Est [1:10](https://ref.ly/Esth1:10), [1:12](https://ref.ly/Esth1:12), [1:15](https://ref.ly/Esth1:15) \+ 9 t. Est; promises made to eunuchs [Is 56:3](https://ref.ly/Isa56:3), [56:4](https://ref.ly/Isa56:4). (Term never used in law codes; on contrary cf. exclusion of פְּחוּעַ דַּכָּא, and כְּרוּת שָׁפְכָה [Dt 23:2](https://ref.ly/Deut23:2) \[v. Drad loc.], also מְרוֺחַ אָשֶׁח [Lv 21:20](https://ref.ly/Lev21:20).)—רַב־סָרִיס is title of high military officer [2 K 18:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:17) (As.), [Je 39:3](https://ref.ly/Jer39:3), [39:13](https://ref.ly/Jer39:13) (Bab.).—Vid. further, Thes SmithDB Art. *‘ Eunuch, ’* Di:Gn 39, 1 Dr Dt23, 1\.

> \[Str5633] I. \[סֶ֫רֶן]:21 **n. m.** tyrant, lord (Philist. loan\-word);—pl. abs. הַסְּרָנִים Ju [16:30](https://ref.ly/Judg16:30) \+ 2 t.; cstr. סַרְנֵי Ju [3:3](https://ref.ly/Judg3:3) \+ 16 t.; sf. סַרְנֵיכֶם [1 S 6:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:4); —only of *tyrants, lords* of the Philistines, five in no. Ju [3:3](https://ref.ly/Judg3:3), [Jos 13:3](https://ref.ly/Josh13:3) (D), [1 S 6:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:16), [6:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:18) cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:4), i.e. appar., one ruling each of the five cities (named [1 S 6:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam6:17)); without the numeral Ju [16:5](https://ref.ly/Judg16:5) \+ 6 t. ## סָרַר (id: BDB06696)

\[Str5637] † סָרַר **vb.** be stubborn, rebellious (usu. towards ´י) (NH *id.*, (rare); As. *sarâru*);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. יִשְרָאֵל ´ס [Ho 4:16](https://ref.ly/Hos4:16) *Isr. is stubborn; Pt. act.* בֵּן סוֺרֵר וּמוֺרֶה [Dt 21:18](https://ref.ly/Deut21:18), cf. v:[20](https://ref.ly/Deut21:20); בָּנִים סוֺרְרִים [Is 30:1](https://ref.ly/Isa30:1), עַם סוֺרֵר [65:2](https://ref.ly/Isa65:2), וּמֹרֶה ´דּוֺר ס [Psalm 78:8](https://ref.ly/Ps78:8) (all of Isr.); וּמוֺרֶה ´לֵב מ [Je 5:23](https://ref.ly/Jer5:23); פָּרָה סֹרֵרָ֔ה [Ho 4:16](https://ref.ly/Hos4:16) (sim. of Isr., v. supr.); as pred. שָרֵיהֶם סוֺרְרִים [Ho 9:15](https://ref.ly/Hos9:15) cf. [Is 1:23](https://ref.ly/Isa1:23); סָרֵי סוֺרְרִים [Je 6:28](https://ref.ly/Jer6:28) *revolters among the rebellious* (? cf. סור **Qal** ad fin., and סַר infr.); of loose woman הֹמִיָּסה הִיא וְסוֺרָרֶת [Pr 7:11](https://ref.ly/Prov7:11); as subst. \= *the stubborn*, [Psalm 66:7](https://ref.ly/Ps66:7), [68:7](https://ref.ly/Ps68:7), [68:19](https://ref.ly/Ps68:19); fs. וַיִּתְּנוּ כָתֵף סֹרֶ֫רֶת [Ne 9:29](https://ref.ly/Neh9:29) *and they presented a stubborn shoulder* (of Isr.) \= [Zc 7:11](https://ref.ly/Zech7:11) (סֹרָרֶת).

> \[Str5620] † סַר **adj.** stubborn, resentful, sullen, implacable;—סַר וְזָעֵף [1 K 20:43](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:43), [21:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:4); f. מַה־זֶּה רוּחֲךָ סָרָ֫ה v:[5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:5) why then *is thy spirit sullen ?* poss. also pl. cstr. סָרֵי סוֺרְרִים [Je 6:28](https://ref.ly/Jer6:28) *revolters* (*stubborn ones*) among, etc. (v. supr. and סור **Qal** ad fin.).

> \[Str5638] סתו Kt, סְתָיו Qr **n. m.** winter (loan\-wd. fr. Aram.; cf. Syr. سَتوْء, Targum סִיתְוָא; Ar. شِتَآءٌ Lane1504; prob. orig. ´ש, cf. SchwallyIdiot. 64; on ו as old nominat. ending (´ס for *sûutayu*) cf. LagBN 190 Anm.);—הַסְּתָ֯ו עָבָר Ct [2:11](https://ref.ly/Song2:11).

## סָתַם (id: BDB06699)

\[Str5640] † סָתַם **vb.** stop up, shut up, keep close (NH *id.;* Targum סְתַם *id.;* Syr. ܣܛܰܡ (ܣܬܡ very rare); Ar. سَدَمَ *close* door Lane1334, and سَطَمَ Kam Frey, are perh. loan\-words);—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´ס [2 Ch 32:30](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:30); *Impf.* 2 mpl. תִּסְתָֹּמוּ [2 K 3:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:19); 3 mpl. יִסְתָֹּמוּ v:[25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:25), וַיִּסְתְּמוּ [2 Ch 32:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:4); *Imv.* ms. סְתֹם [Dn 8:26](https://ref.ly/Dan8:26), [12:4](https://ref.ly/Dan12:4); *Inf. cstr.* לִסְתּוֺם [2 Ch 32:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:3); *Pt. pass.* סָתוּם Ez [28:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:3), סָתֻם [Psalm 51:8](https://ref.ly/Ps51:8), pl. סְתֻמִים [Dn 12:9](https://ref.ly/Dan12:9); —**1\.** *stop up* springs of water [2 K 3:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:19), [3:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:25), [2 Ch 32:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:3), [32:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:4), cf. v:[30](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:30). **2\.** *shut up, keep close*, prophetic words [Dn 8:26](https://ref.ly/Dan8:26), [12:4](https://ref.ly/Dan12:4), [12:9](https://ref.ly/Dan12:9); בְּסָתֻם [Psalm 51:8](https://ref.ly/Ps51:8) *in* (the) *closed* (chamber of the breast; ‖טֻחוֺת).—כָּל־סָתוּם Ez [28:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:3) usu. no *secret* is too dark for thee, but doubtful (v. II. עמם); LXX σοφοί, Co חַרְטֻמִּים \< Toy חֲכָמִים, or Berthol. קֹסְמִים. **Niph.** *Inf. cstr.* לְהִסָּתֵם [Ne 4:1](https://ref.ly/Neh4:1) the breaches \[in the walls] had begun *to be stopped up*. **Pi.** *Pf.* 3 pl. sf. סִתְּמוּם [Gn 26:16](https://ref.ly/Gen26:16); *Impf.* 3 mpl. sf. וַיְסַתְּמוּם v:[18](https://ref.ly/Gen26:18), both of *stopping* wells *quite up* (RJE).

711:a. סָתַם: cf. As. *bit sutummu, storehouse, treasury,* ZehnpfBAS i. 531\.

* **Associated Passages:** Genesis 26:18; Nehemiah 4:1; 2 Kings 3:19; 2 Chronicles 32:30; Daniel 8:26; Daniel 12:4; Ezekiel 28:3; Genesis 26:16; Psalms 51:8; Daniel 12:9; 2 Chronicles 32:4; 2 Chronicles 32:3; 2 Kings 3:25
* **Associated Articles:** II עָמַם (ID: `BDB07183@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## סָתַר (id: BDB06700)

\[Str5641] † סָתַר **vb.** hide, conceal (NH *id.*, der. species; Targum סְתַר Pa. Ithpa., Syr. ܣܬܰܪ Pe. Pt. pass., chiefly Pa. Ithpa., *id.;* Ar. سَتَرَ *veil, conceal, hide*, Lane1304; Eth. ሰተረ፡ (rare) DI354);—**Niph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. נִסְתָּר [Pr 27:12](https://ref.ly/Prov27:12) \+ [22:3](https://ref.ly/Prov22:3) Qr (Kt וַיִּסָּתֵר); 2 ms. נִסְתַּ֫רְתָּ [1 S 20:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:19); 1 pl. נִסְתָּרְנוּ [Is 28:15](https://ref.ly/Isa28:15), etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִסָּתֵר [Ho 13:14](https://ref.ly/Hos13:14) \+; 1 S. אֶסָּתֵר [Gn 4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14) \+; 3 mpl. יִסָּֽתְרוּ [Am 9:3](https://ref.ly/Amos9:3); 1 pl. נִסָּתֵר [Gn 31:49](https://ref.ly/Gen31:49), etc.; *Imv.* ms. הִסָּתֵר [Je 36:19](https://ref.ly/Jer36:19); *Inf. cstr.* לְהִסָּ֫תֶר שָׁם [Jb 34:22](https://ref.ly/Job34:22); *Pt.* נִסְתָּר [Psalm 19:7](https://ref.ly/Ps19:7); pl. נִסְתָּרִים [Dt 7:20](https://ref.ly/Deut7:20); fpl. נִסְתָּרֹת [Dt 29:28](https://ref.ly/Deut29:28), וֺת\- [Psalm 19:13](https://ref.ly/Ps19:13); —**1\.** *hide oneself*, c. בְּ loc., [1 S 20:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:5), [20:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:24), [1 K 17:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:3), [Je 23:24](https://ref.ly/Jer23:24), so (fig.) [Is 28:15](https://ref.ly/Isa28:15); c. שָׁם [1 S 20:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:19), [Jb 34:22](https://ref.ly/Job34:22); abs. [Je 36:19](https://ref.ly/Jer36:19), [Pr 22:3](https://ref.ly/Prov22:3), [27:12](https://ref.ly/Prov27:12), [28:28](https://ref.ly/Prov28:28), [Psalm 89:47](https://ref.ly/Ps89:47); c. מִפְּנֵי Pers. [Dt 7:20](https://ref.ly/Deut7:20), [Jb 13:20](https://ref.ly/Job13:20), c. מִן Pers. [Psalm 55:13](https://ref.ly/Ps55:13). **2\.** *be hid, concealed*, esp. fig. of escaping God's notice, c. מִפְּנֵי Pers. [Gn 4:14](https://ref.ly/Gen4:14) (J), מִלִּפְנֵי [Je 16:17](https://ref.ly/Jer16:17), מִנֶּגֶד עֵינַי [Am 9:3](https://ref.ly/Amos9:3), מֵעֵינַי [Is 65:16](https://ref.ly/Isa65:16), [Ho 13:14](https://ref.ly/Hos13:14) (subj. נֹחַם, i.e. I will not repent, change my purpose of judgment), מִן [Psalm 38:10](https://ref.ly/Ps38:10), [Is 40:27](https://ref.ly/Isa40:27); fr. heat of sun [Psalm 19:7](https://ref.ly/Ps19:7) (i.e. it penetrates everywhere); fr. birds [Jb 28:21](https://ref.ly/Job28:21) (of place of wisdom); recipr. אִישׁ מֵרֵעֵהוּ ´נִסּ [Gn 31:49](https://ref.ly/Gen31:49) (J) when *we are hidden each from the other* (i.e. separated); abs. [Nu 5:13](https://ref.ly/Num5:13), [Jb 3:23](https://ref.ly/Job3:23) *(whose way is hid* sc. from himself), [Zp 2:3](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:3) (i.e. escape ´י's judgment); pt. *hidden, secret things*, in gen. [Dt 29:28](https://ref.ly/Deut29:28); of sins [Psalm 19:13](https://ref.ly/Ps19:13). **Pi.** *Imv.* fs. סַתְּרִי [Is 16:3](https://ref.ly/Isa16:3) *carefully hide* (i.e. *shelter*) the outcasts (Moab speaks to Zion). **Pu.** *Pt.* fs. אַהֲבָה מְסֻתָּרֶת [Pr 27:5](https://ref.ly/Prov27:5) *love carefully concealed*. **Hithp.** *Impf.* 3 fs. תִּסְתַּתָּר [Is 29:14](https://ref.ly/Isa29:14); *Pt.* מִסְתַּתֵּר [1 S 23:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:19) \+ 3 t.;—*hide oneself carefully*, of David's taking refuge with (עִם) Ziphites [1 S 23:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam23:19) \= [Psalm 54:2](https://ref.ly/Ps54:2) (title), in (בְּ) a hill [1 S 26:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam26:1); ´אֵל מ [Is 45:15](https://ref.ly/Isa45:15) *a God that completely hides himself*. **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. הִסְתִּיר [Psalm 10:11](https://ref.ly/Ps10:11), [22:25](https://ref.ly/Ps22:25); 2 ms. הִסְתַּ֫רְתָּ [Psalm 30:8](https://ref.ly/Ps30:8), [Is 64:6](https://ref.ly/Isa64:6); etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יַסְתִּיר [1 S 20:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:2), וַיַּסְתֵּר [Ex 3:6](https://ref.ly/Exod3:6), [Jb 3:10](https://ref.ly/Job3:10), sf. יַסְתִּרֵ֫נִי [Psalm 27:5](https://ref.ly/Ps27:5), וַיַּסְתִּרֵם [Je 36:26](https://ref.ly/Jer36:26); 3 fs. sf. וַתַּסְתִּירֵ֫הוּ [2 Ch 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:11); 1 S. וָאַסְתִּר Ez [39:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:23), [39:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:24), etc.; *Imv.* ms. הַסְתֵּר [Psalm 51:11](https://ref.ly/Ps51:11); *Inf. abs.* הַסְתֵּר [Dt 31:18](https://ref.ly/Deut31:18), [Is 57:17](https://ref.ly/Isa57:17); *cstr.* הַסְתֵּר [Pr 25:2](https://ref.ly/Prov25:2) (v. Köi. 213 and reff.; yet cf. inf. abs. אָכֹל v:[27](https://ref.ly/Prov25:27)), לַסְתִּר [Is 29:15](https://ref.ly/Isa29:15) (Ges§ 53 q); *Pt.* מַסְתִּיר [Is 8:17](https://ref.ly/Isa8:17); —*conceal, hide:* **1\.** a person from (מִפְּנֵי) enemy [2 K 11:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:2) \= [2 Ch 22:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr22:11); subj. ´י, c. מִן [Psalm 64:3](https://ref.ly/Ps64:3), abs. [Je 36:26](https://ref.ly/Jer36:26); *hide* anything from (מִן) one [1 S 20:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:2), a thing, in gen., [Pr 25:2](https://ref.ly/Prov25:2); anything (fr. ´י; מִן in ‖ cl.) [Is 29:15](https://ref.ly/Isa29:15); in gen. \= *shelter* a person [Jb 14:13](https://ref.ly/Job14:13), c. בְּ loc. (subj. ´י) [Psalm 17:8](https://ref.ly/Ps17:8), [27:5](https://ref.ly/Ps27:5), [31:21](https://ref.ly/Ps31:21) (all metaph.); toil (עָמָל), מֵעֵיָ\+ני [Jb 3:10](https://ref.ly/Job3:10); commands fr., מִן Pers. (subj. ´י) [Psalm 119:19](https://ref.ly/Ps119:19); *hide* one as arrow in quiver [Is 49:2](https://ref.ly/Isa49:2). **2\.** esp. *hide* the face: **a.** lit., of Moses [Ex 3:6](https://ref.ly/Exod3:6) (E), usu. fig.: **b.** subj. ´י c. מִן i.e. be not observant of sin [Psalm 51:11](https://ref.ly/Ps51:11), abs. [Psalm 10:11](https://ref.ly/Ps10:11); more oft. **c.** ´י *hides* his face from (מִן Pers.), i.e. withdraws his favour, [Mi 3:4](https://ref.ly/Mic3:4), [Is 8:17](https://ref.ly/Isa8:17), [54:8](https://ref.ly/Isa54:8), [64:6](https://ref.ly/Isa64:6), [Dt 31:17](https://ref.ly/Deut31:17), [32:20](https://ref.ly/Deut32:20), [Je 33:5](https://ref.ly/Jer33:5), Ez [39:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:23), [39:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:24), [39:29](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:29), [Psalm 13:2](https://ref.ly/Ps13:2), [22:25](https://ref.ly/Ps22:25), [27:9](https://ref.ly/Ps27:9), [69:18](https://ref.ly/Ps69:18), [88:15](https://ref.ly/Ps88:15), [102:3](https://ref.ly/Ps102:3), [143:7](https://ref.ly/Ps143:7); abs. [Dt 31:18](https://ref.ly/Deut31:18), [Jb 13:24](https://ref.ly/Job13:24), [34:29](https://ref.ly/Job34:29), [Psalm 30:8](https://ref.ly/Ps30:8) cf. [104:29](https://ref.ly/Ps104:29), [44:25](https://ref.ly/Ps44:25); פָּנִים om. [Is 57:17](https://ref.ly/Isa57:17); cf. [Is 59:2](https://ref.ly/Isa59:2) your sins *have hidden* (his) *face* from (מִן) you; of hiding one's face from (מִן) shame, i.e. avoiding it [Is 50:6](https://ref.ly/Isa50:6) (of servant of ´י).

> \[Str5643] † סֵ֫תֶר **n.\[m.]** covering, hiding\-place, secrecy;—abs. ´ס Ju [3:19](https://ref.ly/Judg3:19) \+, סָתֶר [2 S 12:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:12), [Pr 25:23](https://ref.ly/Prov25:23), סֶָתֶר [Psalm 139:15](https://ref.ly/Ps139:15); cstr. סֵ֫תֶר [1 S 25:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:20) \+; sf. סִתְרוֺ [Psalm 18:12](https://ref.ly/Ps18:12) (ins. also ‖ [2 S 22:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:12) LXX SyrVer Th Bu HPS), סִתְרִי Psalm 199:114; pl. סְתָרִים [Pr 9:17](https://ref.ly/Prov9:17); —**1\.** *covering, cover*, הָהָר ´בְּס [1 S 25:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:20) *under cover of the mt.*, concealed by it; of clouds as covering for ´י (shutting out his view) [Jb 22:14](https://ref.ly/Job22:14), of darkness (i.e. dark clouds) as ´י's *hiding\-place* (in theoph.) [Psalm 18:12](https://ref.ly/Ps18:12) (\= [2 S 22:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:12) v. supr.), so רַָעַם ´ס [81:8](https://ref.ly/Ps81:8) *hiding\-place of thunder;* of adulterer פָּנִים ´ס יָשִים [Jb 24:15](https://ref.ly/Job24:15) i.e. disguiseth himself. **2\. a.** *hidng\-place*, [1 S 19:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam19:2), [Is 28:17](https://ref.ly/Isa28:17) הַמַּדְרֵגָה ´ס Ct [2:14](https://ref.ly/Song2:14) (in fig.; ‖חַגְוֵי הַסֶּלַע), of hippopot. [Jb 40:21](https://ref.ly/Job40:21); fig. of Isr. as shelter to Moab c. מִפְּנֵי [Is 16:6](https://ref.ly/Isa16:6), of ideal Israelite, as זֶרֶם ´ס [32:2](https://ref.ly/Isa32:2), esp. of ´י as *shelter:* אָהֳלוֺ ´ס [Psalm 27:5](https://ref.ly/Ps27:5), פָּנֶיְ ´ס [31:21](https://ref.ly/Ps31:21), כְּנָפֶיְ ´ס [61:5](https://ref.ly/Ps61:5), עֶלְיוֺן ´ס [91:1](https://ref.ly/Ps91:1), לִי ´אַתָּה ס [32:7](https://ref.ly/Ps32:7) cf. [119:114](https://ref.ly/Ps119:114). **b.** *secret place*, of womb [Psalm 139:15](https://ref.ly/Ps139:15) (‖תַּחְתִּיּוֺת אָרֶץ). **3\.** *secrecy:* ´דְּבַר־ס Ju [3:19](https://ref.ly/Judg3:19) *a matter of secrecy*, secret matter; pl. abstr. intens. לֶחֶם סְתָרִים [Pr 9:17](https://ref.ly/Prov9:17) *bread of utter secrecy* (i.e. gained stealthily, ‖מַיִם גְּנוּבִים); לְשֹׁן סָתֶר [25:23](https://ref.ly/Prov25:23) *tongue of secrecy*, i.e. slanderous; elsewhere ´בַּסּ *in secrecy, secretly* [Dt 13:7](https://ref.ly/Deut13:7), [27:15](https://ref.ly/Deut27:15), [27:24](https://ref.ly/Deut27:24), [28:57](https://ref.ly/Deut28:57), [2 S 12:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:12), [Je 37:17](https://ref.ly/Jer37:17), [38:16](https://ref.ly/Jer38:16), [40:15](https://ref.ly/Jer40:15), [Is 45:19](https://ref.ly/Isa45:19), [48:16](https://ref.ly/Isa48:16), [Jb 13:10](https://ref.ly/Job13:10), [31:27](https://ref.ly/Job31:27), [Pr 21:14](https://ref.ly/Prov21:14), [Psalm 101:5](https://ref.ly/Ps101:5).

> \[Str5643] † סִתְרָה **n. f.** shelter, protection, יְהִי עֲלֵיכֶם ´ס [Dt 32:38](https://ref.ly/Deut32:38) (rd. perh. יהיו, cf. Dr) *let them* (the strange gods) *be over you as a shelter*.

> \[Str5639] † סְתוּר **n. pr. m.** a spy, from Asher;—[Nu 13:13](https://ref.ly/Num13:13), LXX Σαθουρ, LXXL Θασουρ.

> \[Str5644] † סִתְרִי **n. pr. m.** a Levite;—[Ex 6:22](https://ref.ly/Exod6:22), LXX Σεγρει, A Σεθρει, LXXL Σετρι.

> \[Str4563] † מִסְתּוֺר **n.\[m.]** place of shelter; abs. מִסְתּוֺר מִזֶּרֶם [Is 4:6](https://ref.ly/Isa4:6) (fig. of ´י's protection; cf. סֵתֶר [Is 32:2](https://ref.ly/Isa32:2)).

> \[Str4565] † מִסְתָּר **n.\[m.]** secret place, hiding place;—abs. ´מ [Psalm 10:9](https://ref.ly/Ps10:9), Hb [3:14](https://ref.ly/Hab3:14); usu. pl. מִסְתָּרִים [Je 13:17](https://ref.ly/Jer13:17) \+ 6 t.; sf. מִסְתָּרָיו [Je 49:10](https://ref.ly/Jer49:10); —**1\.** *secret place(s)*, concealed from view, [Je 13:17](https://ref.ly/Jer13:17); where treasures are stored [Is 45:3](https://ref.ly/Isa45:3). **2\.** *hiding\-place(s):* **a.** for protection [Je 23:24](https://ref.ly/Jer23:24), [49:10](https://ref.ly/Jer49:10). **b.** for perpetration of crime, esp. murder: [Psalm 10:8](https://ref.ly/Ps10:8) (‖מַאֲרָב), sim. of lion v:[9](https://ref.ly/Ps10:9), [17:12](https://ref.ly/Ps17:12), cf. Hb [3:14](https://ref.ly/Hab3:14); [Psalm 64:5](https://ref.ly/Ps64:5); of ´י lying in wait [La 3:10](https://ref.ly/Lam3:10) (as a lion).

> \[Str4564] † מַסְתֵּר **n.\[m.]** hiding, act of hiding;—cstr. וּכְמַסְתֵּר פָּנִים מִמֶּנּוּ [Is 53:3](https://ref.ly/Isa53:3) *and like a hiding of face from him* i.e. like one before whom the face is hidden (e.g. a leper, cf. CheHpt).
